** nov.el
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package! nov
  :mode ("\\.epub\\'" . nov-mode)
  :config
  (when (featurep :system 'windows)
    (let ((unzip (my/git-tool "unzip.exe")))
      (when unzip
        (setq nov-unzip-program unzip)))
    ;; unzip 带中文路径时必须按 ANSI 代码页编码参数，否则 unzip 收到乱码路径、
    ;; 报 "cannot find or open ..." 并以退出码 9 失败。
    ;; 原理见 "Windows: 子进程参数的编码（ANSI 代码页）"。
    (defun my/nov-unzip-ansi-coding (orig-fn &rest args)
      (my/with-ansi-process-coding
        (apply orig-fn args)))
    (advice-add 'nov-unzip-epub :around #'my/nov-unzip-ansi-coding))
  (setq nov-variable-pitch nil)
  ;; 2026-10-24: nov-text-width 绝不能再给整数。shr 会按该宽度把文本物理折行
  ;; (buffer 里落真实换行符), 而 global-visual-line-mode 又按窗口宽度软折一次
  ;; —— 窗口窄于该宽度时同一行被刀切两次, 即 Sean 撞到的"切两截"。
  ;; t = nov 原生 "disable filling" 档: 一段一个逻辑行, 折行全权交给
  ;; visual-line, 窗口多宽都只有一层。
  ;; 顶层也设一份: nov-mode 函数体先渲染第一本书、nov-mode-hook 后跑,
  ;; 只在 hook 里设会漏掉打开 epub 瞬间的第一本书。
  (setq nov-text-width t)

  ;; (defun my/nov-setup ()
  ;;   (let* ((available (font-family-list))
  ;;          (font (or (cl-find-if (lambda (f) (member f available))
  ;;                                '("霞鹜文楷 屏幕阅读版" "Noto Sans SC" "Noto Serif SC"
  ;;                                  "Microsoft YaHei UI" "Microsoft YaHei"))
  ;;                    "Microsoft YaHei")))
  ;;     (face-remap-add-relative 'default :family font :height 140)
  ;;     (face-remap-add-relative 'variable-pitch :family font :height 140)
  ;;     (set-fontset-font t nil (font-spec :family font) nil 'prepend)
  ;;     (message "nov: using font \"%s\"" font))
  ;;   (visual-line-mode 1)
  ;;   (setq nov-text-width 80))
  (defun my/nov-setup ()
    (let* ((font (or (my/first-available-font
                      "霞鹜文楷 屏幕阅读版"  ; 旧版 LXGW 的屏幕阅读版
                      "霞鹜文楷"             ; v1.5xx 起去掉了后缀
                      "LXGW WenKai"
                      "霞鹜文楷等宽"
                      "LXGW WenKai Mono"
                      "Noto Sans SC"
                      "Noto Serif SC"
                      "Microsoft YaHei UI"
                      "Microsoft YaHei")
                     "Microsoft YaHei")))
      (face-remap-add-relative 'default :family font :height 140)
      (face-remap-add-relative 'variable-pitch :family font :height 140)

      ;; 中文字符优先使用这个字体
      (set-fontset-font t 'han (font-spec :family font) nil 'prepend)

      (message "nov: using font \"%s\"" font))
    (visual-line-mode 1)
    ;; 同上: t = 禁 filling, 消灭 shr 物理折行层 (整数 80 会把行切两截)
    (setq nov-text-width t))

  (add-hook 'nov-mode-hook #'my/nov-setup)

  ;; Fix: 部分国产生成器（epubBuilder 等）产出的 OPF 里，<package> 的
  ;; unique-identifier 指向一个既没有 id 属性、内容也为空的 <identifier>。
  ;; nov 会抛 "Unique identifier not found by its name: ..."，而这个错误发生在
  ;; nov-mode 的初始化过程中、被 Emacs 降级吞掉，表现为：书解压成功但 buffer 空白
  ;; （nov-documents 为 0），没有任何报错提示。
  ;; identifier 只当阅读进度的键用，取不到就退回文件名——同一本书每次结果一致。
  (defun my/nov-content-unique-identifier-a (orig-fn content)
    (condition-case nil
        (funcall orig-fn content)
      (error
       (or (let ((id (ignore-errors
                       (car (dom-children
                             (esxml-query "package>metadata>identifier" content))))))
             (and (stringp id) (> (length id) 0) (intern id)))
           (let ((file (buffer-file-name)))
             (and file (intern (file-name-base file))))
           'nov-unknown-identifier))))
  (advice-add 'nov-content-unique-identifier :around
              #'my/nov-content-unique-identifier-a)

  ;; Fix: 又一类畸形 epub —— EPUB 2.0 的 <spine toc="ncx"> 引用 id="ncx"，
  ;; 但 manifest 漏登记 <item id="ncx" .../>（toc.ncx 文件其实在 zip 里）。
  ;; nov 的 nov--content-epub2-files 会抛 "EPUB 2 NCX file not found"，
  ;; 同样被 Emacs 吞掉导致 nov-documents 为 0、buffer 空白（看着像乱码）。
  ;; 兜底：manifest 找不到时直接去解压目录里捞 toc.ncx 用。
  (defun my/nov-content-files-ncx-fallback (orig-fn directory content)
    (condition-case err
        (funcall orig-fn directory content)
      (error
       (let ((msg (error-message-string err)))
         (if (string-match-p "EPUB 2 NCX" msg)
             (let* ((manifest (nov-content-manifest directory content))
                    (spine (nov-content-spine content))
                    (files (delq nil (mapcar (lambda (item) (assq item manifest)) spine)))
                    (ncx (car (directory-files directory t "\\.ncx\\'"))))
               (if ncx
                   (progn
                     (setq nov-toc-id 'ncx)
                     (cons (cons 'ncx ncx) files))
                 (signal (car err) (cdr err))))
           (signal (car err) (cdr err)))))))
  (advice-add 'nov-content-files :around #'my/nov-content-files-ncx-fallback)

  ;; Fix: nov-save-place 中文路径 utf-8
  (defun my/nov-save-place-a (orig-fn &rest args)
    (let ((coding-system-for-write 'utf-8))
      (apply orig-fn args)))
  (advice-add 'nov-save-place :around #'my/nov-save-place-a))
#+end_src
** org-noter
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package! org-noter
  :commands org-noter
  :init
  ;; 不读 DJVU：本机没装 djvu 包，org-noter-djvu 加载时会往 echo area 刷
  ;; "ATTENTION: org-noter-djvu needs the package `djvu'"。
  ;; 必须在 org-noter 被 require 之前设置——org-noter.el 顶层就按这个变量
  ;; 决定是否 (require 'org-noter-djvu)，放进 :config 已经晚了。
  (setq org-noter-supported-modes '(doc-view-mode pdf-view-mode nov-mode))
  :config
  ;; 2026-10-18 退役: 根目录 notes/ 空壳 (denote 时代遗留) 随主文件体系一起废弃
  ;; (早已清空, 且不在 agenda / consult-notes / roam 的任何扫描范围内)。
  ;; 2026-10-24: roam/ 改名 notes/ 复用此名 —— 现在的 notes/ 就是笔记池本身。
  ;; PDF 笔记落 notes/ref/ 下的 roam 节点, 复用全库同一套 ID 与 slug 生成器。
  (setq org-noter-notes-search-path (list (expand-file-name "notes/ref/" org-directory))
        org-noter-default-notes-file-names '("notes.org")
        org-noter-auto-save-last-location t
        org-noter-highlight-selected-text t)

  (defun my/org-noter-create-session-in-roam (&optional arg document-file-name)
    "Create an org-noter session whose notes file is a proper roam node.
文件落在 =notes/ref/<id>--<slug>.org=, 头格式遵循 org-roam 的一条硬约束:
**:PROPERTIES: 抽屉必须在 `#+title:' 之前** —— 否则 `org-roam-db-insert-file-node'
在 point 1 判不到 `org-id-get', 文件不会被当成 file node (血泪参见
`org-roam-node-format.md')。"
    (let* ((document-file-name (or (run-hook-with-args-until-success
                                    'org-noter-get-buffer-file-name-hook major-mode)
                                   document-file-name))
           (document-path (or document-file-name buffer-file-truename
                              (error "This buffer does not seem to be visiting any file")))
           (document-name (file-name-nondirectory document-path))
           (document-base (file-name-base document-name))
           (notes-dir (expand-file-name "notes/ref/" org-directory))
          ;; 2026-10-18: 全库 :ID: 已统一为 ts 型时间戳（org-id-method 'ts,
          ;; 形态 %Y%m%dT%H%M%S），与文件名、CUSTOM_ID 三者字面一致。
          ;; 2026-10-23 修: 文件名原先另起 format-time-string 且漏了 T ——
          ;; 注释写着默认模板带 T、代码不带（10-23 全库加 T 时漏改），
          ;; 现直接拿 id 当前缀，与 `my/literature--node' 同一口径。
          (id (org-id-new))
          ;; 2026-10-24 改: 单横线 `-' → 双横线 `--', 理由同 literature 的
          ;; `my/literature--node' —— 单横线文件名不被 denote 认作笔记。
          ;; 落点在 notes/ref/, 属于 denote-directory 内, 必须合规。
          (notes-file (expand-file-name
                       (concat id "--"
                               ;; 2026-10-24: org-roam 退役 → 自家复刻的 slug
                               (my/sluggify-title document-base)
                               ".org")
                       notes-dir)))
      (make-directory notes-dir t)
      (unless (file-exists-p notes-file)
        (with-temp-file notes-file
          (insert (format
                   (concat ":PROPERTIES:\n"
                           ":ID:       %s\n"
                           ":CUSTOM_ID: %s\n"
                           ":END:\n"
                           "#+title:      %s\n"
                           "#+filetags:   :ref:\n"
                           "#+created:    %s\n\n"
                           "* %s\n"
                           ":PROPERTIES:\n"
                           ":NOTER_DOCUMENT: %s\n"
                           ":END:\n")
                   id id
                   document-base
                   (format-time-string "[%Y-%m-%d %a]")
                   document-base
                   (expand-file-name document-path)))))
      (with-current-buffer (find-file-noselect notes-file)
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (re-search-forward (org-re-property org-noter-property-doc-file) nil t)
        (org-back-to-heading t)
        (org-noter))))

  (setq org-noter-create-session-from-document-hook
        '(my/org-noter-create-session-in-roam)))
#+end_src

** epub-reader (yibie)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; epub-reader: 走 TextUI 框架的原生 EPUB 阅读器
;; - 仓库: github.com/yibie/epub-reader (作者 chenyibin)
;; - 卖点: CJK 友好的 reflow、按章节首屏渲染、内置书签/高亮/笔记、可折叠 TOC
;; - 入口: M-x epub-reader-open (命令式, 不挂 auto-mode-alist; 默认 .epub 仍归 nov)
;; - 不在 MELPA, packages.el 已通过 straight 直装 GitHub
;; - 系统依赖: unzip 或 bsdtar (Emacs 用 executable-find 找)
;;   Windows 上 git 内置 unzip.exe 在 Git for Windows 的 usr/bin,
;;   Emacs 默认 exec-path 找不到, 下面用 advice 把 git usr/bin 兜底进去。
(use-package! epub-reader
  :commands (epub-reader-open epub-reader-quit)
  :config
  ;; ---- 阅读观感 ----
  ;; 字号微调: text-scale=0 用 base font; 改大嫌小改小嫌大时调这里
  (setq epub-reader-text-scale 1
        ;; 默认段落间 1 个空行; 中文阅读更舒服可调到 2
        epub-reader-paragraph-spacing 1
        epub-reader-line-spacing 0.15
        ;; 打开书籍时独占当前 frame; 想要和 org 并列就 nil
        epub-reader-open-full-frame t
        ;; 进度/书签/高亮存 sidecar, 默认放 EPUB 同目录
        ;; 想集中管理: (setq epub-reader-store-directory "~/org/notes/books/.epub-reader/")
        epub-reader-enable-progress t
        ;; 列宽: 默认 76; Windows 上 22pt JetBrains Mono + CJK 混排 70-80 都行
        epub-reader-reading-width 76)

  ;; ---- Windows: 把 git 内置 unzip 暴露给 container adapter finder ----
  ;; epub-reader-container--find-program 只查 executable-find,
  ;; 默认只搜 "unzip" / "bsdtar", Git for Windows 的 unzip.exe 不在 PATH
  ;; 上, Emacs exec-path 也找不到. advice 后找不到时 fallback 到 git usr/bin.
  (when (featurep :system 'windows)
    (defun my/epub-reader--find-program-a (orig-fn adapter)
      (or (funcall orig-fn adapter)
          (and my/git-usr-bin
               (executable-find
                (expand-file-name (concat (symbol-name adapter) ".exe")
                                  my/git-usr-bin)))))
    (advice-add 'epub-reader-container--find-program
                :around #'my/epub-reader--find-program-a)

    ;; ---- Windows: 中文路径走 ANSI 代码页 ----
    ;; 同 nov 的坑: zip 流式解压用 `unzip -p ARCHIVE MEMBER`, ARCHIVE 是中文
    ;; 路径时会被 Windows 按 ANSI 代码页解码, 跟我们的 UTF-8 偏好冲突。
    ;; 包 epub-reader-container-open 整个调用, 让里面的 call-process 都走 ANSI。
    (defun my/epub-reader-container-open-ansi-a (orig-fn &rest args)
      (my/with-ansi-process-coding
        (apply orig-fn args)))
    (advice-add 'epub-reader-container-open
                :around #'my/epub-reader-container-open-ansi-a))

  ;; ---- CJK 字体设置 (复用 nov 的字体优先级) ----
  (defun my/epub-reader-setup ()
    (let* ((font (or (my/first-available-font
                      "霞鹜文楷 屏幕阅读版"  ; 旧版 LXGW
                      "霞鹜文楷"             ; v1.5xx 起去后缀
                      "LXGW WenKai"
                      "霞鹜文楷等宽"
                      "LXGW WenKai Mono"
                      "Sarasa Mono SC"
                      "Noto Sans SC"
                      "Noto Serif SC"
                      "Microsoft YaHei UI"
                      "Microsoft YaHei")
                     "Microsoft YaHei")))
      (face-remap-add-relative 'default :family font :height 140)
      (face-remap-add-relative 'variable-pitch :family font :height 140)
      (set-fontset-font t 'han (font-spec :family font) nil 'prepend)
      (message "epub-reader: using font \"%s\"" font)))
  ;; 模式启用后立刻调; 模式没有专门的 hook 公开,
  ;; 用 minor-mode 的 after-change-major-mode-hook 兜底也行, 但更稳的是
  ;; advice epub-reader-open, 保证打开即生效。
  (defun my/epub-reader-open-font-a (orig-fn &rest args)
    (prog1 (apply orig-fn args)
      (when (derived-mode-p 'textui-mode)  ; epub-reader-ui-mode 基于 textui
        (my/epub-reader-setup))))
  (advice-add 'epub-reader-open :around #'my/epub-reader-open-font-a)

  ;; ---- DWIM: 当前 buffer 是 EPUB / dired 中选中 / 提示输入 ----
  (defun my/epub-reader-open-dwim (&optional file)
    "Open EPUB file with epub-reader.
Use FILE arg, current buffer's file (if .epub), or prompt."
    (interactive)
    (let ((target (or file
                      (and (derived-mode-p 'nov-mode) buffer-file-name)
                      (and (derived-mode-p 'textui-mode) buffer-file-name)
                      (and (eq major-mode 'dired-mode)
                           (or (dired-get-file-for-visit)
                               (car (dired-get-marked-files
                                     nil (lambda (f)
                                           (string-suffix-p ".epub" f))))))
                      (read-file-name "Open EPUB: " nil nil t
                                      nil (lambda (f) (string-suffix-p ".epub" f))))))
      (unless target (user-error "No EPUB file selected"))
      (epub-reader-open (expand-file-name target)))))
#+end_src

* Investment
** beancount
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; beancount: 复式记账 / 投资记录
;; - 安装 CLI:
;;   pip install beancount fava fava_investor
;;   pip install git+https://github.com/PhracturedBlue/fava-portfolio-summary@7d3301619025d735830c389948e5527456050d44
;; - 主账本放在 ~/org/investment/ledger/main.beancount（入口文件，只放 option/include）
;; - 正式交易/现金流用下面的 `my/beancount-record-*` 命令直接入账；org-capture 只做信息不完整时的暂存。
;; - 通过 fava (浏览器 UI) 查看报表: fava ~/org/investment/ledger/main.beancount
(use-package! beancount
  :mode ("\\.beancount\\'" . beancount-mode)
  :config
  (setq beancount-electric-currency t
        beancount-use-ido nil))           ; 用 vertico 接管补全

;; 账本根目录：2026-09-24 重构后流水都在 investment/ledger/ 下
;; （accounts/ 账户声明、prices/ 价格、transactions/ 流水、main.beancount 入口）
(defconst my/beancount-ledger-dir
  (expand-file-name "investment/ledger/" org-directory)
  "投资账本根目录：main.beancount 所在目录。")

(defun my/beancount-main-file ()
  "Return the main investment Beancount ledger path.
2026-09-24 目录重构后它是「入口文件」——只有 option / fava 扩展 / query / include，
不再直接承载交易。追加流水请用 `my/beancount-year-file'。"
  (expand-file-name "main.beancount" my/beancount-ledger-dir))

(defun my/beancount-year-file (&optional date)
  "Return the per-year transaction file for DATE (a \"YYYY-MM-DD\" string).
约定：transactions/ 下一年一个文件，不再按月拆。DATE 为空则取当前年份。
文件不存在时自动创建，并把 include 行登记进 main.beancount。"
  (let* ((year (if (and date (string-match "\\`\\([0-9]\\{4\\}\\)-" date))
                   (match-string 1 date)
                 (format-time-string "%Y")))
         (file (expand-file-name (format "transactions/%s.beancount" year)
                                 my/beancount-ledger-dir)))
    (unless (file-exists-p file)
      (my/beancount--init-year-file year file))
    file))

(defun my/beancount--init-year-file (year file)
  "Create YEAR's transaction file FILE and register it in main.beancount."
  (with-temp-buffer
    (insert (format "; -*- mode: beancount; -*-\n\n;; %s 年流水\n" year))
    (write-region (point-min) (point-max) file))
  (with-current-buffer (find-file-noselect (my/beancount-main-file))
    (save-excursion
      (goto-char (point-min))
      (let (last)
        (while (re-search-forward "^include \"transactions/[0-9]\\{4\\}\\.beancount\"" nil t)
          (setq last (point)))
        (if last
            (progn (goto-char last) (forward-line 1)
                   (insert (format "include \"transactions/%s.beancount\"\n" year)))
          (goto-char (point-max))
          (insert (format "\ninclude \"transactions/%s.beancount\"\n" year)))))
    (save-buffer))
  (message "已创建 %s 并登记进 main.beancount"
           (file-relative-name file my/beancount-ledger-dir)))

(defun my/beancount-manual-price-file ()
  "Return the hand-maintained price file."
  (expand-file-name "prices/manual.beancount" my/beancount-ledger-dir))

(defun my/beancount-balances-file ()
  "Return the centralized balance-assertion file."
  (expand-file-name "transactions/balances.beancount" my/beancount-ledger-dir))


;; rustledger LSP：实时诊断 / 账户与收款人补全 / hover 显示余额 / 重命名重构
;;   二进制：Linux ~/.local/bin/rledger-lsp（v0.24.0）；Windows: scoop install rustledger
;;   定位是「第二引擎」——只做校验与编辑增强；展示仍以 fava 为准
;;   （rustledger 的 report 目前不做多商品折算，净资产视图别用它）
;;   不想用就 (setq my/beancount-eglot nil)
(defvar my/beancount-eglot t
  "是否在 beancount-mode 里启用 rledger-lsp。")
(when (and my/beancount-eglot (executable-find "rledger-lsp"))
  (require 'eglot)
  ;; 必须显式把根账本路径传给 LSP：账本在 ~/org/investment/ 子目录里，
  ;; 而 eglot 交给 LSP 的 rootUri 是 project 根（~/org，因为有 .git），
  ;; rledger-lsp 在 ~/org 下自动发现不到 main.beancount，就会退化成「单文件诊断」——
  ;; 月度流水文件里没有 open 指令，于是满屏 "Account ... was never opened"。
  ;; （2026-09-18 账本从 ~/beancounta 迁到 ~/org/investment 后才暴露：
  ;;  以前工作区根就是账本根，自动发现能命中，所以一直没出问题。）
  (add-to-list 'eglot-server-programs
               `(beancount-mode . ("rledger-lsp"
                                   :initializationOptions
                                   (:journalFile ,(expand-file-name "investment/ledger/main.beancount"
                                                                    org-directory)))))
  (add-hook 'beancount-mode-hook #'eglot-ensure))

(require 'subr-x)
(require 'cl-lib)

(defconst my/beancount-funds
  '(("159501" :name "嘉实纳斯达克100ETF(QDII)" :commodity "ETF159501"
     :account "Assets:Investment:Broker:Normal:Nasdaq100:ETF159501" :category "纳指100")
    ("159659" :name "招商纳斯达克100ETF(QDII)" :commodity "ETF159659"
     :account "Assets:Investment:Broker:Normal:Nasdaq100:ETF159659" :category "纳指100")
    ("159696" :name "易方达纳斯达克100ETF(QDII)" :commodity "ETF159696"
     :account "Assets:Investment:Broker:Normal:Nasdaq100:ETF159696" :category "纳指100")
    ("513100" :name "国泰纳斯达克100ETF(QDII)" :commodity "ETF513100"
     :account "Assets:Investment:Broker:Normal:Nasdaq100:ETF513100" :category "纳指100")
    ("513870" :name "富国纳斯达克100ETF(QDII)" :commodity "ETF513870"
     :account "Assets:Investment:Broker:Normal:Nasdaq100:ETF513870" :category "纳指100")
    ("511880" :name "银华日利ETF" :commodity "ETF511880"
     :account "Assets:Investment:Broker:Normal:MoneyMarket:ETF511880" :category "货币基金" :fee-free t))
  "投资账本中的基金/ETF 映射。")

(defconst my/beancount-broker-accounts '("普通账户" "信用账户")
  "Broker account labels used by investment Beancount captures.")

(defun my/beancount--read-broker-account (&optional prompt default)
  "Read a broker account label with PROMPT and DEFAULT."
  (completing-read (or prompt "账户: ")
                   my/beancount-broker-accounts nil t nil nil
                   (or default "普通账户")))

(defun my/beancount--broker-account-alias (token)
  "Return a broker account label when TOKEN names one, otherwise nil."
  (pcase (downcase (string-trim token))
    ((or "普通" "普通账户" "normal") "普通账户")
    ((or "信用" "信用账户" "margin") "信用账户")
    (_ nil)))

(defun my/beancount--broker-cash-account (broker-account)
  "Return the Beancount cash account for BROKER-ACCOUNT."
  (pcase broker-account
    ("普通账户" "Assets:Investment:Broker:Normal:Cash")
    ("信用账户" "Assets:Investment:Broker:Margin:Cash")
    (_ (user-error "Unknown broker account: %s" broker-account))))

(defun my/beancount--broker-fund-account (fund broker-account)
  "Return the Beancount holding account for FUND in BROKER-ACCOUNT."
  (let ((normal-account (plist-get fund :account)))
    (pcase broker-account
      ("普通账户" normal-account)
      ("信用账户"
       (replace-regexp-in-string ":Broker:Normal:" ":Broker:Margin:" normal-account t t))
      (_ (user-error "Unknown broker account: %s" broker-account)))))

(defconst my/beancount-bank-wealth
  '(("DYB" :name "招行多月宝" :commodity "WMDYB"
     :account "Assets:Investment:BankWealth:DYB" :category "银行理财" :fee-free t)
    ("DQB" :name "招行定期宝" :commodity "WMDQB"

(defun my/beancount--bank-wealth-candidates ()
  "Return completion candidates for bank wealth products."
  (mapcar (lambda (cell)
            (let* ((code (car cell))
                   (fund (cdr cell))
                   (name (plist-get fund :name)))
              (cons (format "%s · %s" code name) code)))
          my/beancount-bank-wealth))

(defun my/beancount--pension-candidates ()
  "Return completion candidates for pension funds."
  (mapcar (lambda (cell)
            (let* ((code (car cell))
                   (fund (cdr cell))
                   (name (plist-get fund :name)))
              (cons (format "%s · %s" code name) code)))
          my/beancount-pension-funds))

(defun my/beancount--bank-wealth-fund (code)
  "Return plist for bank wealth CODE."
  (or (cdr (assoc code my/beancount-bank-wealth))
      (user-error "Unknown bank wealth product: %s" code)))

(defun my/beancount--pension-fund (code)
  "Return plist for pension fund CODE."
  (or (cdr (assoc code my/beancount-pension-funds))
      (user-error "Unknown pension fund: %s" code)))

(defun my/beancount--append-bank-wealth-entry (entry &optional date code)
  "Append ENTRY to the year transaction file for bank wealth products."
  (find-file (my/beancount-year-file date))
  (goto-char (point-max))
  (unless (bolp) (insert "\n"))
  (insert "\n" entry)
  (save-buffer)
  (my/beancount-check-main))

(defun my/beancount-record-bank-redeem ()
  "快速记录招行理财（多月宝/定期宝）赎回。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date))
         (code (let* ((candidates (my/beancount--bank-wealth-candidates))
                     (choice (completing-read "理财产品: " candidates nil t)))
                 (cdr (assoc choice candidates))))
         (fund (my/beancount--bank-wealth-fund code))
         (name (plist-get fund :name))
         (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity))
         (account (plist-get fund :account))
         (amount (my/beancount--read-number-string "赎回金额: "))
         (shares (my/beancount--read-number-string "赎回份额: "))
         (price-input (read-string "赎回净值(可空，自动=金额/份额): "))
         (price (if (string-empty-p price-input)
                    (my/beancount--normalize-number
                     (format "%.4f" (/ (my/beancount--number amount)
                                       (my/beancount--number shares))))
                  price-input))
         (cost-price-raw (read-string "成本价(必填): "))
         (cost-price (my/beancount--normalize-number cost-price-raw))
         (cost-date-input (read-string "成本日期(可空，留空仅按成本价匹配): "))
         (cost-lot (if (string-empty-p cost-date-input)
                       (format "{%s CNY}" cost-price)
                     (format "{%s CNY, %s}" cost-price cost-date-input))))
    (when (string-empty-p cost-price)
      (user-error "银行理财赎回需要成本价，用来匹配 Beancount lot"))
    (my/beancount--append-bank-wealth-entry
     (format "%s * \"赎回 %s (%s)\"\n  product_code: \"%s\"\n  product_name: \"%s\"\n  asset_category: \"银行理财\"\n  trade_price: \"%s CNY\"\n  trade_value: \"%s CNY\"\n  %s  -%s %s %s\n  Assets:Bank:CMB:Cash  %s CNY\n  Income:Investment:CapitalGains\n"
             date name code code name price amount
             account shares commodity cost-lot amount)
     date code)))

(defun my/beancount-record-pension-buy ()
  "快速记录个人养老金基金定投买入。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date))
         (code (let* ((candidates (my/beancount--pension-candidates))
                     (choice (completing-read "养老金基金: " candidates nil t)))
                 (cdr (assoc choice candidates))))
         (fund (my/beancount--pension-fund code))
         (name (plist-get fund :name))
         (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity))
         (account (plist-get fund :account))
         (shares (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交份数: "))
         (price (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交净值: "))
         (fee (my/beancount--read-number-string "手续费: " "0"))
         (num-shares (my/beancount--number shares))
         (num-price (my/beancount--number price))
         (num-fee (my/beancount--number fee))
         (lot-cost (my/beancount--normalize-number
                    (format "%.2f" (* num-shares num-price))))
         (trade-value (my/beancount--normalize-number
                       (format "%.2f" (+ (my/beancount--number lot-cost) num-fee)))))
    (my/beancount--append-bank-wealth-entry
     (format "%s * \"定投买入 %s (%s)\"\n  fund_code: \"%s\"\n  fund_name: \"%s\"\n  asset_category: \"个人养老金\"\n  trade_price: \"%s CNY\"\n  trade_value: \"%s CNY\"\n  %s  %s %s {{%s CNY}}\n%s  Assets:Investment:Pension:Cash\n"
             date name code code name price trade-value
             account (my/beancount--normalize-number shares) commodity lot-cost
             (if (> num-fee 0)
                 (format "  Expenses:Investment:Fees  %s CNY\n" fee)
               ""))
     date code)))


(defun my/open-beancount-main ()
  "打开主 beancount 账本。"
  (interactive)
  (find-file (my/beancount-main-file)))

(defun my/beancount--fund-candidates ()
  "Return completion candidates for `my/beancount-funds`."
  (mapcar (lambda (cell)
            (let* ((code (car cell))
                   (fund (cdr cell))
                   (name (plist-get fund :name)))
              (cons (format "%s · %s" code name) code)))
          my/beancount-funds))

(defun my/beancount--read-fund-code ()
  "Read a fund code from known Beancount fund mappings."
  (let* ((candidates (my/beancount--fund-candidates))
         (choice (completing-read "基金代码: " candidates nil t)))
    (cdr (assoc choice candidates))))

(defun my/beancount--price-candidates ()
  "Return completion candidates for price capture (funds + bank wealth)."
  (append
   (mapcar (lambda (cell)
             (let* ((code (car cell))
                    (fund (cdr cell))
                    (name (plist-get fund :name))
                    (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity)))
               (cons (format "基金 %s · %s" code name)
                     (list :type 'fund :code code :commodity commodity))))
           my/beancount-funds)
   (mapcar (lambda (cell)
             (let* ((code (car cell))
                    (fund (cdr cell))
                    (name (plist-get fund :name))
                    (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity)))
               (cons (format "理财 %s · %s" code name)
                     (list :type 'bank-wealth :code code :commodity commodity))))
           my/beancount-bank-wealth)))

(defun my/beancount--read-price-target ()
  "Read an instrument target for price capture."
  (let* ((candidates (my/beancount--price-candidates))
         (choice (completing-read "价格标的: " candidates nil t)))
    (or (cdr (assoc choice candidates))
        (user-error "Unknown price target: %s" choice))))

(defun my/beancount--fund (code)
  "Return plist for CODE."
  (or (cdr (assoc code my/beancount-funds))
      (user-error "Unknown fund code: %s" code)))

(defun my/beancount--normalize-number (value)
  "Normalize numeric string VALUE for Beancount."
  (replace-regexp-in-string "," "" (string-trim (format "%s" value))))

(defun my/beancount--number (value)
  "Convert VALUE to number after normalizing commas."
  (string-to-number (my/beancount--normalize-number value)))

(defun my/beancount--read-number-string (prompt &optional default)
  "Read a decimal number as normalized string with PROMPT and optional DEFAULT."
  (my/beancount--normalize-number
   (read-string prompt nil nil default)))

(defun my/beancount--holding-instrument (code)
  "Return holding instrument plist for CODE across ETFs, pension funds, and wealth."
  (or (cdr (assoc code my/beancount-funds))
      (cdr (assoc code my/beancount-pension-funds))
      (cdr (assoc code my/beancount-bank-wealth))
      (cdr (assoc code my/beancount-cash-accounts))
      (cdr (assoc code my/beancount-other-balances))
      (user-error "Unknown holding code: %s" code)))

(defun my/beancount--parse-balance-batch-line (line line-number broker-account)
  "Parse LINE at LINE-NUMBER into a balance posting plist.
ETF lines use BROKER-ACCOUNT unless LINE starts with a broker account alias."
  (let ((trimmed (string-trim line)))
    (unless (or (string-empty-p trimmed)
                (string-prefix-p "#" trimmed)
                (string-prefix-p ";" trimmed))
      (let (line-broker-account code shares)
        (cond
         ((and (string-match "\\`\\([^[:space:],，]+\\)[[:space:],，]+\\([^[:space:],，]+\\)[[:space:],，]+\\(.+\\)\\'" trimmed)
               (setq line-broker-account
                     (my/beancount--broker-account-alias (match-string 1 trimmed))))
          (setq code (match-string 2 trimmed)
                shares (match-string 3 trimmed)))
         ((string-match "\\`\\([^[:space:],，]+\\)[[:space:],，]+\\(.+\\)\\'" trimmed)
          (when (my/beancount--broker-account-alias (match-string 1 trimmed))
            (user-error "Line %d should be: ACCOUNT CODE SHARES" line-number))
          (setq code (match-string 1 trimmed)
                shares (match-string 2 trimmed)))
         (t
          (user-error "Line %d should be: CODE SHARES or ACCOUNT CODE SHARES" line-number)))
        (let* ((shares (my/beancount--normalize-number shares))
               (instrument (my/beancount--holding-instrument code))
               (is-etf (assoc code my/beancount-funds))
               (account (if is-etf
                            (my/beancount--broker-fund-account
                             instrument (or line-broker-account broker-account))
                          (plist-get instrument :account)))
               (commodity (plist-get instrument :commodity)))
          (when (and line-broker-account (not is-etf))
            (user-error "Line %d has broker account prefix for non-ETF code: %s"
                        line-number code))
          (unless (string-match-p "\\`[-+]?[0-9]+\\(?:\\.[0-9]+\\)?\\'" shares)
            (user-error "Line %d has invalid shares/amount: %s" line-number shares))
          (list :code code :shares shares :account account :commodity commodity))))))

(defun my/beancount--balance-batch-build (date text &optional broker-account)
  "Build a Beancount balance snapshot for DATE from pasted TEXT."
  (let (postings)
    (cl-loop for line in (split-string text "\n")
             for line-number from 1
             for parsed = (my/beancount--parse-balance-batch-line
                           line line-number (or broker-account "普通账户"))
             when parsed
             do (push (format "%s balance %s %s %s"
                              date
                              (plist-get parsed :account)
                              (plist-get parsed :shares)
                              (plist-get parsed :commodity))
                      postings))
    (unless postings
      (user-error "No holdings found. Paste lines like: 513870 1234.56"))
    (list :count (length postings)
          :entry (mapconcat #'identity
                            (cons (format "; ---- Holdings balance snapshot %s ----" date)
                                  (nreverse postings))
                            "\n"))))

(defconst my/beancount-etf-commission-rate 0.00005
  "ETF commission rate for the current broker: 0.5 per 10,000.")

(defconst my/beancount-etf-min-commission 5.0
  "Minimum ETF commission in CNY for the current broker.")

(defun my/beancount--etf-commission (fund trade-value)
  "Return ETF commission for FUND and TRADE-VALUE."
  (if (plist-get fund :fee-free)
      0
    (max my/beancount-etf-min-commission
         (* trade-value my/beancount-etf-commission-rate))))

(defun my/beancount--read-date (&optional prompt)
  "Read a Beancount date string."
  (read-string (or prompt "交易日期: ") (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d")))

(defun my/beancount--safe-string (value)
  "Return VALUE trimmed and safe for a Beancount quoted string."
  (replace-regexp-in-string "\"" "'" (string-trim value)))

(defun my/beancount--insert-before-first-entry (text)
  "Insert TEXT before the first price/transaction/balance entry, else at EOF."
  (goto-char (point-min))
  (if (re-search-forward "^[0-9]\{4\}-[0-9]\{2\}-[0-9]\{2\} \\(price\\|balance\\|[*!]\\)" nil t)
      (progn
        (beginning-of-line)
        (insert text "\n"))
    (goto-char (point-max))
    (unless (bolp) (insert "\n"))
    (insert text "\n")))

(defun my/beancount--book-files ()
  "Return every .beancount file under the investment book."
  (directory-files-recursively my/beancount-ledger-dir "\\.beancount\\'"))

(defun my/beancount--declared-in-book-p (regexp)
  "Return non-nil if REGEXP matches a directive anywhere in the book."
  (catch 'hit
    (dolist (f (my/beancount--book-files))
      (with-temp-buffer
        (insert-file-contents f)
        (when (re-search-forward regexp nil t) (throw 'hit t))))))

(defun my/beancount--append-directive (file text)
  "Append TEXT as a new directive at the end of FILE."
  (with-current-buffer (find-file-noselect file)
    (save-excursion
      (goto-char (point-max))
      (unless (bolp) (insert "\n"))
      (insert "\n" text "\n"))
    (save-buffer)))

(defun my/beancount--ensure-fund-open (date code &optional account)
  "Ensure commodity and account directives for CODE exist, using DATE if inserted.
When ACCOUNT is non-nil, ensure that account instead of the default normal account.
搜索范围是整本账（重构后 commodity/open 都在 ledger/accounts/ 下，不在流水文件里）；
缺失时写到对应的声明文件，而不是当前流水文件，避免污染入口 / 重复声明。"
  (let* ((fund (my/beancount--fund code))
         (name (plist-get fund :name))
         (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity))
         (account (or account (plist-get fund :account)))
         (category (plist-get fund :category)))
    (unless (my/beancount--declared-in-book-p
             (format "^[0-9-]+ commodity %s\\b" (regexp-quote commodity)))
      (my/beancount--append-directive
       (expand-file-name "accounts/commodities.beancount" my/beancount-ledger-dir)
       (format "%s commodity %s\n  name: \"%s\"\n  code: \"%s\"\n  asset_category: \"%s\""
               date commodity name code category)))
    (unless (my/beancount--declared-in-book-p
             (format "^[0-9-]+ open %s\\b" (regexp-quote account)))
      (my/beancount--append-directive
       (expand-file-name "accounts/assets.beancount" my/beancount-ledger-dir)
       (format "%s open %s %s" date account commodity)))))

(defun my/beancount--append-entry (entry &optional date code account file)
  "Append ENTRY to FILE (default: the year transaction file for DATE).
Optionally ensures CODE is open at DATE."
  (find-file (or file (my/beancount-year-file date)))
  (when (and date code)
    (my/beancount--ensure-fund-open date code account))
  (goto-char (point-max))
  (unless (bolp) (insert "\n"))
  (insert "\n" entry)
  (save-buffer)
  (my/beancount-check-main))

(defun my/beancount-check-main ()
  "Run bean-check against the main Beancount ledger."
  (interactive)
  (let ((file (my/beancount-main-file))
        (program (my/beancount-script "bean-check")))
    (save-some-buffers t (lambda ()
                           (and buffer-file-name
                                (file-equal-p buffer-file-name file))))
    (compilation-start
     (format "\"%s\" \"%s\"" program file)
     nil
     (lambda (_) "*bean-check main.beancount*"))))

(defun my/beancount-record-cmb-cashflow ()
  "快速记录招行现金账户的转入或转出。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date))
         (direction (completing-read "方向: " '("转入" "转出") nil t))
         (out-target (when (string= direction "转出")
                       (completing-read "转出到: " '("股票账户" "养老金账户" "消费" "还信用卡") nil t)))
         (cashflow-type (if (string= direction "转入")
                            "转入"
                          (format "转出:%s" out-target)))
         (amount (my/beancount--read-number-string "金额: "))
         (summary (my/beancount--safe-string (read-string "摘要: ")))
         (note (my/beancount--safe-string (read-string "备注(可空): ")))
         (action-label (if (string= direction "转入")
                           "转入"
                         (format "转出到%s" out-target)))
         (title (if (string-empty-p summary)
                    (format "招行现金%s" action-label)
                  (format "招行现金%s %s" action-label summary)))
         (cash-posting (if (string= direction "转入")
                           (format "  Assets:Bank:CMB:Cash  %s CNY\n" amount)
                         (format "  Assets:Bank:CMB:Cash  -%s CNY\n" amount)))
         (counter-posting (cond
                            ((string= direction "转入")
                             "  Income:ExternalFunding\n")
                            ((string= out-target "股票账户")
                             (format "  Assets:Investment:Broker:Normal:Cash  %s CNY\n" amount))
                            ((string= out-target "养老金账户")
                             (format "  Assets:Investment:Pension:Cash  %s CNY\n" amount))
                            ((string= out-target "消费")
                             "  Expenses:ExternalOutflow\n")
                            ((string= out-target "还信用卡")
                             "  Expenses:ExternalOutflow\n")))
         (target-line (if (and out-target (not (string-empty-p out-target)))
                          (format "  out_target: \"%s\"\n" out-target)
                        ""))
         (note-line (if (string-empty-p note)
                        ""
                      (format "  note: \"%s\"\n" note))))
    (my/beancount--append-entry
     (format "%s * \"%s\"\n  cashflow_type: \"%s\"\n%s  trade_value: \"%s CNY\"\n%s%s%s"
             date title cashflow-type target-line amount note-line cash-posting counter-posting))))

(defconst my/beancount-bank-interest-accounts
  '(("CMB" :name "招行活期现金" :commodity "CNY"
     :account "Assets:Bank:CMB:Cash"
     :income "Income:Investment:BankInterest"))
  "银行现金账户的存款结息映射：现金账户 + 对应的利息收入科目。")

(defun my/beancount--bank-interest-candidates ()
  "Return completion candidates for bank interest sources."
  (mapcar (lambda (cell)
            (cons (format "%s · %s" (car cell) (plist-get (cdr cell) :name)) (car cell)))
          my/beancount-bank-interest-accounts))

(defun my/beancount-record-bank-interest ()
  "快速记录银行现金账户的存款利息收入（如招行活期季度结息）。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date))
         (cands (my/beancount--bank-interest-candidates))
         (code (if (= 1 (length cands))
                   (caar my/beancount-bank-interest-accounts)
                 (let ((choice (completing-read "结息账户: " cands nil t)))
                   (or (cdr (assoc choice cands)) choice))))
         (spec (or (cdr (assoc code my/beancount-bank-interest-accounts))
                   (user-error "Unknown bank interest account: %s" code)))
         (name (plist-get spec :name))
         (account (plist-get spec :account))
         (income (plist-get spec :income))
         (amount (my/beancount--read-number-string "利息金额: "))
         (note (my/beancount--safe-string (read-string "备注(可空): ")))
         (title (format "%s结息" name))
         (note-line (if (string-empty-p note) "" (format "  note: \"%s\"\n" note))))
    (my/beancount--append-entry
     (format "%s * \"%s\"\n  interest_type: \"BankInterest\"\n  bank: \"%s\"\n  trade_value: \"%s CNY\"\n%s  %s  %s CNY\n  %s  -%s CNY\n"
             date title code amount note-line account amount income amount))))


(defun my/beancount-record-buy ()
  "快速记录一笔 ETF/基金买入。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date))
         (code (my/beancount--read-fund-code))
         (fund (my/beancount--fund code))
         (name (plist-get fund :name))
         (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity))
         (broker-account "普通账户")
         (account (my/beancount--broker-fund-account fund broker-account))
         (cash-account (my/beancount--broker-cash-account broker-account))
         (category (plist-get fund :category))
         (price (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交价格: "))
         (shares (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交份额: "))
         (amount (* (my/beancount--number price) (my/beancount--number shares)))
         (fee (my/beancount--etf-commission fund amount)))
    (my/beancount--append-entry
     (format "%s * \"买入 %s (%s)\"\n  fund_code: \"%s\"\n  fund_name: \"%s\"\n  asset_category: \"%s\"\n  trade_price: \"%s CNY\"\n  trade_value: \"%.2f CNY\"\n  %s  %s %s {{%.2f CNY}}\n%s  %s\n"
             date name code code name category price amount
             account shares commodity amount
             (if (> fee 0)
                 (format "  Expenses:Investment:Fees  %.4f CNY\n" fee)
               "")
             cash-account)
     date code account)))

(defun my/beancount-record-sell ()
  "快速记录一笔 ETF/基金卖出。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date))
         (code (my/beancount--read-fund-code))
         (fund (my/beancount--fund code))
         (name (plist-get fund :name))
         (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity))
         (broker-account "普通账户")
         (account (my/beancount--broker-fund-account fund broker-account))
         (cash-account (my/beancount--broker-cash-account broker-account))
         (category (plist-get fund :category))
         (price (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交价格: "))
         (shares (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交份额: "))
         (cost-price (read-string "成本价(可空，例 100.427): "))
         (cost-date (unless (string= cost-price "")
                      (my/beancount--read-date "成本日期: ")))
         (cost-lot (if (string= cost-price "")
                       "{}"
                     (format "{%s CNY, %s}" cost-price cost-date)))
         (trade-value (* (my/beancount--number price) (my/beancount--number shares)))
         (fee (my/beancount--etf-commission fund trade-value))
         (cash-received (- trade-value fee)))
    (my/beancount--append-entry
     (format "%s * \"卖出 %s (%s)\"\n  fund_code: \"%s\"\n  fund_name: \"%s\"\n  asset_category: \"%s\"\n  trade_price: \"%s CNY\"\n  trade_value: \"%.2f CNY\"\n  %s  -%s %s %s @ %s CNY\n%s  %s  %.2f CNY\n  Income:Investment:CapitalGains\n"
             date name code code name category price trade-value
             account shares commodity cost-lot price
             (if (> fee 0)
                 (format "  Expenses:Investment:Fees  %.2f CNY\n" fee)
               "")
             cash-account
             cash-received)
     date code account)))

(defun my/beancount-record-collateral-transfer ()
  "快速记录普通账户和信用账户之间的担保品划转。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date))
         (direction (completing-read "划转方向: "
                                     '("普通账户 -> 信用账户" "信用账户 -> 普通账户")
                                     nil t))
         (from-broker (if (string-prefix-p "普通账户" direction)
                          "普通账户"
                        "信用账户"))
         (to-broker (if (string= from-broker "普通账户")
                        "信用账户"
                      "普通账户"))
         (code (my/beancount--read-fund-code))
         (fund (my/beancount--fund code))
         (name (plist-get fund :name))
         (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity))
         (from-account (my/beancount--broker-fund-account fund from-broker))
         (to-account (my/beancount--broker-fund-account fund to-broker))
         (shares (my/beancount--read-number-string "划转份额: "))
         (cost-price (my/beancount--read-number-string "成本价(用于匹配 lot): "))
         (cost-date (my/beancount--read-date "成本日期: "))
         (summary (my/beancount--safe-string (read-string "摘要(可空): ")))
         (title (if (string-empty-p summary)
                    (format "担保品划转 %s %s (%s)" direction name code)
                  (format "担保品划转 %s %s" direction summary)))
         (cost-lot (format "{%s CNY, %s}" cost-price cost-date)))
    (my/beancount--append-entry
     (format "%s * \"%s\"\n  transfer_type: \"担保品划转\"\n  broker_from: \"%s\"\n  broker_to: \"%s\"\n  fund_code: \"%s\"\n  fund_name: \"%s\"\n  %s  -%s %s %s\n  %s  %s %s %s\n"
             date title from-broker to-broker code name
             from-account shares commodity cost-lot
             to-account shares commodity cost-lot)
     date code to-account)))

(defun my/beancount-record-margin-buy ()
  "快速记录信用账户融资买入 ETF/基金。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date))
         (code (my/beancount--read-fund-code))
         (fund (my/beancount--fund code))
         (name (plist-get fund :name))
         (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity))
         (account (my/beancount--broker-fund-account fund "信用账户"))
         (category (plist-get fund :category))
         (price (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交价格: "))
         (shares (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交份额: "))
         (amount (* (my/beancount--number price) (my/beancount--number shares)))
         (fee (my/beancount--etf-commission fund amount))
         (debt (+ amount fee)))
    (my/beancount--append-entry
     (format "%s * \"融资买入 %s (%s)\"\n  trade_type: \"融资买入\"\n  fund_code: \"%s\"\n  fund_name: \"%s\"\n  asset_category: \"%s\"\n  trade_price: \"%s CNY\"\n  trade_value: \"%.2f CNY\"\n  %s  %s %s {{%.2f CNY}}\n%s  Liabilities:Investment:Broker:Margin:Principal  -%.2f CNY\n"
             date name code code name category price amount
             account shares commodity amount
             (if (> fee 0)
                 (format "  Expenses:Investment:Fees  %.4f CNY\n" fee)
               "")
             debt)
     date code account)))

(defun my/beancount-record-margin-sell ()
  "快速记录信用账户卖出 ETF/基金，可选择卖出所得偿还融资负债或留在信用现金。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date))
         (code (my/beancount--read-fund-code))
         (fund (my/beancount--fund code))
         (name (plist-get fund :name))
         (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity))
         (account (my/beancount--broker-fund-account fund "信用账户"))
         (category (plist-get fund :category))
         (price (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交价格: "))
         (shares (my/beancount--read-number-string "成交份额: "))
         (cost-price (read-string "成本价(可空，例 100.427): "))
         (cost-date (unless (string= cost-price "")
                      (my/beancount--read-date "成本日期: ")))
         (cost-lot (if (string= cost-price "")
                       "{}"
                     (format "{%s CNY, %s}" cost-price cost-date)))
         (proceeds-target (completing-read "卖出资金去向: "
                                           '("偿还融资负债" "留在信用现金")
                                           nil t nil nil "偿还融资负债"))
         (trade-value (* (my/beancount--number price) (my/beancount--number shares)))
         (fee (my/beancount--etf-commission fund trade-value))
         (cash-received (- trade-value fee))
         (counter-posting (if (string= proceeds-target "偿还融资负债")
                              (format "  Liabilities:Investment:Broker:Margin:Principal  %.2f CNY\n" cash-received)
                            (format "  Assets:Investment:Broker:Margin:Cash  %.2f CNY\n" cash-received))))
    (my/beancount--append-entry
     (format "%s * \"信用账户卖出 %s (%s)\"\n  trade_type: \"%s\"\n  fund_code: \"%s\"\n  fund_name: \"%s\"\n  asset_category: \"%s\"\n  trade_price: \"%s CNY\"\n  trade_value: \"%.2f CNY\"\n  proceeds_target: \"%s\"\n  %s  -%s %s %s @ %s CNY\n%s%s  Income:Investment:CapitalGains\n"
             date name code
             (if (string= proceeds-target "偿还融资负债") "融资卖出还款" "信用账户卖出")
             code name category price trade-value proceeds-target
             account shares commodity cost-lot price
             (if (> fee 0)
                 (format "  Expenses:Investment:Fees  %.2f CNY\n" fee)
               "")
             counter-posting)
     date code account)))

(defun my/beancount-record-price ()
  "快速记录一条 ETF/基金/理财净值 price。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date "净值日期: "))
         (target (my/beancount--read-price-target))
         (commodity (plist-get target :commodity))
         (price (my/beancount--read-number-string "当前净值: "))
         (ensure-code (when (eq (plist-get target :type) 'fund)
                        (plist-get target :code))))
    ;; 手工净值写 ledger/prices/manual.beancount（auto.beancount 是脚本生成的，别手改）
    (my/beancount--append-entry
     (format "%s price %s %s CNY\n" date commodity price)
     date ensure-code nil (my/beancount-manual-price-file))))

;;;; 批量录价：每日净值一行，插入后手工改数字
;; 只放**拿不到公开行情**的标的。P022907/P022918/P022945 三只养老金基金由
;; tools/update_prices.py（东财 NAV）自动写进 prices/auto.beancount，走 SPC n i u，
;; 这里再录一遍就会和 auto 形成同日重复 price。招行定期宝没有公开接口，只能手工抄净值。

(defconst my/beancount-price-batch-targets
  '("DQB")
  "批量录价（SPC n i p）的标的：只有拿不到公开行情的（招行定期宝）。")

(defun my/beancount--manual-price-text ()
  "Return the text of the manual price file, preferring a live buffer.
优先读已打开的 buffer，这样上一次还没保存的录入也能被算作「已录过」。"
  (let* ((file (my/beancount-manual-price-file))
         (buf (get-file-buffer file)))
    (cond (buf (with-current-buffer buf (save-restriction (widen) (buffer-string))))
          ((file-exists-p file)
           (with-temp-buffer (insert-file-contents file) (buffer-string))))))

(defun my/beancount--last-price (commodity)
  "Return (DATE . PRICE) for the last manual price directive of COMMODITY."
  (let ((text (my/beancount--manual-price-text)))
    (when text
      (with-temp-buffer
        (insert text)
        (goto-char (point-max))
        (when (re-search-backward
               (format "^\\([0-9]\\{4\\}-[0-9]\\{2\\}-[0-9]\\{2\\}\\) price %s +\\([0-9.]+\\)"
                       (regexp-quote commodity))
               nil t)
          (cons (match-string 1) (match-string 2)))))))

(defun my/beancount--price-on-date (date commodity)
  "Return the manual price already recorded for COMMODITY on DATE, else nil."
  (let ((text (my/beancount--manual-price-text)))
    (when text
      (with-temp-buffer
        (insert text)
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (when (re-search-forward
               (format "^%s price %s +\\([0-9.]+\\)"
                       (regexp-quote date) (regexp-quote commodity))
               nil t)
          (match-string 1))))))

(defun my/beancount--price-batch-line (date code &optional width)
  "Build one manual `price' line for CODE on DATE.
价格预填上一次手工记录的净值，行尾注释写明沿用日期，提醒要改。
WIDTH 用来把商品代码左对齐，保证四行的价格数字在同一列（方便 C-n 往下改）。"
  (let* ((plist (my/beancount--holding-instrument code))
         (commodity (plist-get plist :commodity))
         (name (plist-get plist :name))
         (width (or width (length commodity)))
         (last (my/beancount--last-price commodity))
         (value (if last (cdr last) "0.0000"))
         (note (if last (format "沿用 %s，记得改" (car last)) "无历史值，请填写")))
    (format "%s price %s %s CNY  ; %s (%s)"
            date
            (truncate-string-to-width commodity width nil ?\s)
            value name note)))

(define-minor-mode my/beancount-price-batch-mode
  "Minor mode for a freshly inserted batch of `price' lines.
`C-c C-c' 保存文件并跑 bean-check。"
  :lighter " PriceBatch"
  :keymap (let ((map (make-sparse-keymap)))
            (define-key map (kbd "C-c C-c") #'my/beancount-price-batch-commit)
            map))

(defun my/beancount-price-batch-commit ()
  "Save the batch price file, leave review mode, and run bean-check."
  (interactive)
  (my/beancount-price-batch-mode -1)
  (save-buffer)
  (my/beancount-check-main))

(defun my/beancount-record-price-batch ()
  "一次性生成当天待录净值（`my/beancount-price-batch-targets`）并打开 buffer 改价格。
价格预填上期值，光标落在第一行价格上，C-n 沿列往下改；改完 C-c C-c 保存并 bean-check。
同一天同一标的已经录过则跳过（全部录过时直接报错提示）。
ETF 与养老金基金的净值不要在这里录，走 SPC n i u 自动抓。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date "净值日期: "))
         (width (apply #'max
                       (mapcar (lambda (code)
                                 (length (plist-get (my/beancount--holding-instrument code)
                                                    :commodity)))
                               my/beancount-price-batch-targets)))
         lines existing beg)
    (dolist (code my/beancount-price-batch-targets)
      (let ((commodity (plist-get (my/beancount--holding-instrument code) :commodity)))
        (if (my/beancount--price-on-date date commodity)
            (push commodity existing)
          (push (my/beancount--price-batch-line date code width) lines))))
    (setq lines (nreverse lines)
          existing (nreverse existing))
    (unless lines
      (user-error "%s 这批净值都已录入：%s" date (string-join existing " ")))
    (find-file (my/beancount-manual-price-file))
    (goto-char (point-max))
    (unless (bolp) (insert "\n"))
    (setq beg (point))
    (insert "\n" (mapconcat #'identity lines "\n") "\n")
    (my/beancount-price-batch-mode 1)
    ;; 光标停在第一行价格的数字开头，方便直接改
    (goto-char beg)
    (re-search-forward "price \\(CNY\\|[A-Z0-9]+\\) +" nil t)
    (message "%d 行待改%s；改完 C-c C-c 保存并 bean-check"
             (length lines)
             (if existing (format "（已跳过已录入的 %s）" (string-join existing " ")) ""))))

(defun my/beancount-record-balance ()
  "快速记录一条 ETF/基金持仓 balance 校验。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((date (my/beancount--read-date "校验日期: "))
         (code (my/beancount--read-fund-code))
         (fund (my/beancount--fund code))
         (broker-account (my/beancount--read-broker-account "校验账户: " "信用账户"))
         (commodity (plist-get fund :commodity))
         (account (my/beancount--broker-fund-account fund broker-account))
         (shares (my/beancount--read-number-string "持有份额/股数(不是基金代码): ")))
    (when (string= shares code)
      (user-error "这里要填持有份额/股数，不是基金代码；%s 当前应填实际剩余份额" code))
    ;; balance 断言集中放 ledger/transactions/balances.beancount（与 apply-balances.py 输出一致）
    (my/beancount--append-entry
     (format "%s balance %s %s %s\n" date account shares commodity)
     date code account (my/beancount-balances-file))))

(defvar-local my/beancount-balance-batch-date nil
  "Balance date for the current Beancount batch balance buffer.")

(defvar-local my/beancount-balance-batch-broker-account nil
  "Broker account for ETF lines in the current Beancount batch balance buffer.")

(defvar my/beancount-balance-batch-mode-map
  (let ((map (make-sparse-keymap)))
    (define-key map (kbd "C-c C-c") #'my/beancount-balance-batch-submit)
    (define-key map (kbd "C-c C-k") #'kill-current-buffer)
    map)
  "Keymap for `my/beancount-balance-batch-mode'.")

(define-derived-mode my/beancount-balance-batch-mode text-mode "BeanBalanceBatch"
  "Major mode for pasting Beancount holding balances.")

(defun my/beancount-record-balance-batch ()
  "Paste multiple ETF, pension, and bank-wealth balances into a buffer."
  (interactive)
  (let ((date (my/beancount--read-date "校验日期: "))
        (broker-account (my/beancount--read-broker-account "ETF 校验账户: " "信用账户"))
        (buffer (get-buffer-create "*beancount holding balances*")))
    (pop-to-buffer buffer)
    (my/beancount-balance-batch-mode)
    (setq-local my/beancount-balance-batch-date date)
    (setq-local my/beancount-balance-batch-broker-account broker-account)
    (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
      (erase-buffer)
      (insert "# Paste holdings as CODE SHARES, one per line.\n")
      (insert (format "# ETF default account: %s.\n" broker-account))
      (insert "# ETF lines may override it with: 普通账户 CODE SHARES or 信用账户 CODE SHARES.\n")
      (insert "# Supported: ETF codes, pension fund codes, DYB, DQB, CMB (招行活期现金), CMB:LOAN (招行贷款).\n")
      (insert "# Submit with C-c C-c. Cancel with C-c C-k.\n")
      (insert "# Examples:\n")
      (insert "# 信用账户 513870 1234\n")
      (insert "# 普通账户 511880 1000\n")
      (insert "# 022907 62.14\n")
      (insert "# DYB 10000\n")
      (insert "# CMB 1283.38\n\n"))))

(defun my/beancount-balance-batch-submit ()
  "Append the current batch balance buffer to the main Beancount ledger."
  (interactive)
  (unless my/beancount-balance-batch-date
    (user-error "This buffer was not created by my/beancount-record-balance-batch"))
  (let* ((source-buffer (current-buffer))
         (date my/beancount-balance-batch-date)
         (broker-account (or my/beancount-balance-batch-broker-account "普通账户"))
         (built (my/beancount--balance-batch-build
                 date
                 (buffer-substring-no-properties (point-min) (point-max))
                 broker-account))
         (count (plist-get built :count))
         (entry (plist-get built :entry)))
    (my/beancount--append-entry entry nil nil nil (my/beancount-balances-file))
    (when (buffer-live-p source-buffer)
      (kill-buffer source-buffer))
    (message "Inserted %d balance checks for %s" count date)))

(defconst my/beancount-fava-port 5000
  "fava 监听端口；与 investment/.vscode/tasks.json 里的 --port 保持一致。")

(defun my/beancount-fava-url ()
  "fava 首页 URL。直接访问 / 即可，fava 会自己 302 到 /<slug>/，不用拼 slug。"
  (format "http://localhost:%d/" my/beancount-fava-port))

(defun my/beancount-fava-live-p ()
  "fava 端口是否已在监听（Emacs 起的或别处起的都算）。"
  (condition-case nil
      (let ((conn (open-network-stream "fava-probe" nil "localhost" my/beancount-fava-port)))
        (delete-process conn)
        t)
    (error nil)))

(defun my/beancount-fava--open-when-ready (&optional tries)
  "轮询等 fava 端口就绪后打开浏览器；TRIES 是已尝试次数（0.5s 一次，最多 15 秒）。"
  (let ((tries (or tries 0)))
    (cond ((my/beancount-fava-live-p)
           (browse-url (my/beancount-fava-url))
           (message "fava 已就绪：%s" (my/beancount-fava-url)))
          ((< tries 30)
           (run-with-timer 0.5 nil #'my/beancount-fava--open-when-ready (1+ tries)))
          (t
           (pop-to-buffer "*fava*")
           (message "fava 15 秒仍未就绪，见 *fava* buffer")))))

(defun my/beancount-fava-main ()
  "打开主账本，确保 fava 在跑，然后在浏览器里打开报表。
已实测（2026-09-18）：fava_dashboards 的 dashboards.yaml 是按账本文件所在目录解析的，
把 cwd 设成 /tmp 起 fava，6 个 panel 照样全过，所以 cwd 不影响 dashboards。
这里仍显式绑 default-directory，只是防止它落在 tramp / 已删除目录时子进程启动异常。"
  (interactive)
  (my/open-beancount-main)
  (let ((default-directory (file-name-directory (my/beancount-main-file))))
    (if (my/beancount-fava-live-p)
        (progn
          (browse-url (my/beancount-fava-url))
          (message "复用已在运行的 fava：%s" (my/beancount-fava-url)))
      (start-process "fava" "*fava*"
                     (my/beancount-script "fava")
                     "--port" (number-to-string my/beancount-fava-port)
                     (my/beancount-main-file))
      (my/beancount-fava--open-when-ready))))

(defun my/beancount-python-executable ()
  "Return a Python executable with the investment script dependencies.
Linux 优先用 ~/.venvs/beancount（uv 建的 venv，装了 beancount/fava 全套）；
Windows 用 scoop python311；都找不到才退回 PATH 上的 python3/python。"
  (let ((candidates (list (expand-file-name ".venvs/beancount/bin/python" "~")
                          (expand-file-name "scoop/apps/python311/current/python.exe" "~")
                          (executable-find "python3")
                          (executable-find "python"))))
    (catch 'found
      (dolist (candidate candidates)
        (when (and candidate (file-executable-p candidate))
          (throw 'found candidate)))
      "python")))

(defun my/beancount-script (name)
  "Resolve the absolute path of a Beancount console script NAME (e.g. \"bean-check\", \"fava\").
The scoop Python that installs beancount/fava puts its Scripts directory
(scoop/persist/python311/Scripts) outside PATH, so `executable-find' alone fails.
Fall back to the script sitting next to `my/beancount-python-executable'."
  (or (executable-find name)
      (let* ((py (my/beancount-python-executable))
             (py-dir (and (file-name-absolute-p py)
                          (file-name-directory (expand-file-name py))))
             (cands (when py-dir
                      (list (expand-file-name (concat "Scripts/" name ".exe") py-dir)
                            (expand-file-name (concat "Scripts/" name) py-dir)
                            (expand-file-name name py-dir)))))
        (catch 'found
          (dolist (cand cands)
            (when (file-exists-p cand) (throw 'found cand)))
          name))))

(defun my/beancount-update-prices-latest ()
  "抓取最近两周 ETF 收盘价，更新 ledger/prices/auto.beancount。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((script (expand-file-name "investment/tools/update_prices.py" org-directory))
         (default-directory (expand-file-name "investment/" org-directory)))
    (compilation-start
     (format "%s %s latest"
             (shell-quote-argument (my/beancount-python-executable))
             (shell-quote-argument script))
     nil
     (lambda (_) "*beancount update prices latest*"))))

(defun my/beancount-rebuild-prices-history ()
  "重建全部 ETF 历史收盘价 ledger/prices/auto.beancount；建议每周跑一次。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((script (expand-file-name "investment/tools/update_prices.py" org-directory))
         (default-directory (expand-file-name "investment/" org-directory)))
    (compilation-start
     (format "%s %s history"
             (shell-quote-argument (my/beancount-python-executable))
             (shell-quote-argument script))
     nil
     (lambda (_) "*beancount rebuild prices history*"))))

#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun my/eshell-here ()
  "Open a new eshell rooted at the current buffer's directory."
  (interactive)
  (let ((default-directory (or (and buffer-file-name
                                    (file-name-directory buffer-file-name))
                               default-directory)))
    (eshell t)))

(defun eshell/doom (&rest args)
  "Run Doom Emacs CLI from eshell.
Invokes the current Emacs executable in batch mode with the doom script,
identical to what `doom.ps1' does on Windows."
  (let ((doom-script (expand-file-name "~/.emacs.d/bin/doom"))
        (emacs-bin (expand-file-name invocation-name invocation-directory)))
    (if (file-exists-p doom-script)
        (apply #'call-process emacs-bin nil t nil
               "-q" "--no-site-file" "--batch"
               "--load" doom-script "--" "--no-color"
               args)
      (error "Doom script not found at %s" doom-script))))

** Elfeed
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package! elfeed
  :commands elfeed
  :config
  (require 'subr-x)
  (setq elfeed-db-directory (expand-file-name "~/org/.elfeed")
        elfeed-curl-max-connections 4)
  (setq-default elfeed-search-filter "+unread")
  ;; 2026-10-23: 文章窗从底部改到右侧, 实现「左列表 / 右文章」左右分屏。
  ;; 根因不是 elfeed 原生行为, 而是 Doom rss 模块的 popup 规则 ——
  ;; 它在 display-buffer-alist 里把 *elfeed-entry* 钉死在
  ;; display-buffer-below-selected (side bottom)。elfeed 4.x 的显示链路是
  ;; elfeed-show-entry-switch (pop-to-buffer) -> display-buffer -> 命中该规则,
  ;; 所以改 split-width-threshold / elfeed-show-entry-switch 都没用。
  ;; 这里用同名规则覆盖它 (Doom 的 set-popup-rule! 会替换同 regexp 的旧条目)。
  ;; 2026-10-23 装了 elfeed-goodies 后此规则退居二线: goodies 的 setup 会把
  ;; entry-switch 换成 switch-pane (popwin 窗口管理, 不经 display-buffer),
  ;; 实际布局改由 elfeed-goodies/entry-pane-position 决定 (同为 right/0.55)。
  ;; 保留本规则作兜底: 卸载 goodies 时它自动重新接管。
  (set-popup-rule! "*elfeed-entry*" :side 'right :size 0.55 :select t :quit t)
  ;; 避免每次进入 elfeed 都疯狂触发后台更新导致界面跳动。
  ;; 建议手动按 r / gr 更新，或者使用定时器。
  ;; (add-hook 'elfeed-search-mode-hook #'elfeed-update)

  (defun my/elfeed-show-buffer-setup ()
    "Apply local display settings for Elfeed article buffers."
    (setq-local shr-use-fonts nil)
    (setq-local line-spacing 0))

  (defun my/elfeed-select-show-window (&rest _)
    "Keep focus in the Elfeed article pane after show-buffer navigation."
    (when-let ((window (cl-find-if
                        (lambda (window)
                          (with-current-buffer (window-buffer window)
                            (derived-mode-p 'elfeed-show-mode)))
                        (window-list nil 'no-minibuf))))
      (select-window window)))

  (add-hook 'elfeed-show-mode-hook #'my/elfeed-show-buffer-setup)
  (unless (advice-member-p #'my/elfeed-select-show-window 'elfeed-show-next)
    (advice-add 'elfeed-show-next :after #'my/elfeed-select-show-window))
  (unless (advice-member-p #'my/elfeed-select-show-window 'elfeed-show-prev)
    (advice-add 'elfeed-show-prev :after #'my/elfeed-select-show-window))

  ;; Avoid expensive reload/save on exit when Elfeed DB is not initialized.
  (defun my/elfeed-db-save-if-initialized (orig-fn &rest args)
    (cond
     ((null elfeed-db)
      (message "Skip elfeed-db-save: elfeed-db is not initialized in this session."))
     ((not (plistp elfeed-db))
      (message "Skip elfeed-db-save: invalid elfeed-db (%S)." elfeed-db))
     (t
      (apply orig-fn args))))
  (when (advice-member-p #'my/elfeed-db-sanitize-before-save 'elfeed-db-save)
    (advice-remove 'elfeed-db-save #'my/elfeed-db-sanitize-before-save))
  (unless (advice-member-p #'my/elfeed-db-save-if-initialized 'elfeed-db-save)
    (advice-add 'elfeed-db-save :around #'my/elfeed-db-save-if-initialized))
  (remove-hook 'kill-emacs-hook #'elfeed-db-gc-safe)

  (defvar my/elfeed-feed-history nil
    "Minibuffer history for feed selection commands.")

  (defun my/elfeed-current-entry ()
    "Return the current Elfeed entry in search or show buffers."
    (cond
     ((derived-mode-p 'elfeed-show-mode) elfeed-show-entry)
     ((derived-mode-p 'elfeed-search-mode) (elfeed-search-selected :ignore-region))
     (t nil)))

  (defun my/elfeed-feed-display-title (feed)
    (or (elfeed-meta feed :title)
        (elfeed-feed-title feed)
        (elfeed-feed-url feed)
        "Unknown feed"))

  (defun my/elfeed-url-origin (url)
    (when (and url (string-match "\\`\\(https?://[^/]+\\)" url))
      (concat (match-string 1 url) "/")))

  (defun my/elfeed-guess-homepage (feed-url)
    (when feed-url
      (let ((homepage (car (split-string feed-url "[?#]" t))))
        (dolist (pattern '("/index\\.xml/?$"
                           "/atom\\.xml/?$"
                           "/rss\\.xml/?$"
                           "/feed\\.xml/?$"
                           "/feeds?/[^/]+\\(?:\\.xml\\|\\.atom\\|\\.rss\\)?/?$"
                           "/\\(?:feed\\|rss\\|atom\\)/?$"))
          (setq homepage (replace-regexp-in-string pattern "/" homepage t t)))
        (unless (string-match-p "/$" homepage)
          (setq homepage
                (replace-regexp-in-string "/[^/]+\\(?:\\.xml\\|\\.rss\\|\\.atom\\)?$"
                                          "/"
                                          homepage
                                          t
                                          t)))
        (replace-regexp-in-string "/+$" "/" homepage t t))))

  (defun my/elfeed-entry-homepage (entry)
    (when-let* ((feed (elfeed-entry-feed entry))
                (feed-url (elfeed-feed-url feed)))
      (let* ((entry-url (elfeed-entry-link entry))
             (feed-origin (my/elfeed-url-origin feed-url))
             (entry-origin (my/elfeed-url-origin entry-url)))
        (cond
         ((and entry-origin feed-origin (not (string= entry-origin feed-origin)))
          entry-origin)
         (t
          (or (my/elfeed-guess-homepage feed-url)
              entry-origin
              feed-origin))))))

  (defun my/elfeed-browse-homepage ()
    "Open the homepage of the current entry's feed."
    (interactive)
    (if-let ((entry (my/elfeed-current-entry)))
        (if-let ((homepage (my/elfeed-entry-homepage entry)))
            (browse-url homepage)
          (user-error "Could not infer a homepage for this feed"))
      (user-error "No Elfeed entry at point")))

  (defun my/elfeed-toggle-unread ()
    "Toggle the `unread' tag on the current Elfeed entry or entries."
    (interactive)
    (cond
     ((derived-mode-p 'elfeed-search-mode)
      (elfeed-search-toggle-all 'unread))
     ((derived-mode-p 'elfeed-show-mode)
      (if (elfeed-tagged-p 'unread elfeed-show-entry)
          (elfeed-show-untag 'unread)
        (elfeed-show-tag 'unread)))
     (t
      (user-error "Not in an Elfeed buffer"))))

  (defun my/elfeed-feed-candidates ()
    "获取订阅源列表。注意：在文章数极多（几万条以上）时，
`with-elfeed-db-visit` 遍历全库可能会导致 C-c f 出现几秒卡顿。
届时可换用纯列表极速版：
  (let (candidates)
    (elfeed-db-ensure)
    (maphash
     (lambda (url feed)
       (push (cons (format \"%s\\t%s\" (my/elfeed-feed-display-title feed) url) url)
             candidates))
     elfeed-db-feeds)
    (sort candidates (lambda (a b) (string-lessp (car a) (car b)))))"
    (let ((table (make-hash-table :test 'equal)))
      (with-elfeed-db-visit (entry feed)
        (let* ((url (elfeed-feed-url feed))
               (title (my/elfeed-feed-display-title feed))
               (current (or (gethash url table)
                            (list :url url :title title :all 0 :unread 0)))
               (all (1+ (plist-get current :all)))
               (unread (if (elfeed-tagged-p 'unread entry)
                           (1+ (plist-get current :unread))
                         (plist-get current :unread))))
          (puthash url
                   (list :url url :title title :all all :unread unread)
                   table)))
      (sort
       (let (candidates)
         (maphash
          (lambda (url data)
            (push (cons (format "[%d/%d] %s\t%s"
                                (plist-get data :unread)
                                (plist-get data :all)
                                (plist-get data :title)
                                url)
                        url)
                  candidates))
          table)
         candidates)
       (lambda (a b) (string-lessp (car a) (car b))))))

  (defun my/elfeed-read-feed-url ()
    (let* ((all-feeds "[All feeds]")
           (candidates (my/elfeed-feed-candidates))
           (choices (cons all-feeds (mapcar #'car candidates)))
           (choice (if (require 'consult nil t)
                       (consult--read
                        choices
                        :prompt "Feed: "
                        :sort nil
                        :require-match t
                        :history 'my/elfeed-feed-history)
                     (completing-read "Feed: " choices nil t nil 'my/elfeed-feed-history))))
      (unless (equal choice all-feeds)
        (cdr (assoc choice candidates)))))

  (defun my/elfeed-replace-feed-filter (filter feed-url)
    (let ((parts (cl-remove-if (lambda (part) (string-prefix-p "=" part))
                               (split-string (string-trim (or filter "")) "[ \t\n]+" t))))
      (string-join
       (append parts
               (when feed-url
                 (list (concat "=" (regexp-quote feed-url)))))
       " ")))

  (defun my/elfeed-search-filter-by-feed ()
    "Use `consult' or `completing-read' to filter Elfeed by feed."
    (interactive)
    (unless (derived-mode-p 'elfeed-search-mode)
      (user-error "Feed filtering is only available in elfeed-search"))
    (let* ((feed-url (my/elfeed-read-feed-url))
           (new-filter (my/elfeed-replace-feed-filter elfeed-search-filter feed-url)))
      (elfeed-search-set-filter new-filter)
      (message (if feed-url
                   "Filtered Elfeed to %s"
                 "Cleared feed-specific filter")
               (or feed-url "all feeds"))))

  ;; mpv integration
  (defun my/elfeed-play-with-mpv ()
    "Play the current elfeed entry with mpv."
    (interactive)
    (let ((link (if (derived-mode-p 'elfeed-show-mode)
                    (elfeed-entry-link elfeed-show-entry)
                  (let ((entries (elfeed-search-selected)))
                    (when entries
                      (elfeed-entry-link (car entries)))))))
      (if link
          (progn
            (message "Starting mpv for %s..." link)
            (start-process "elfeed-mpv" nil "mpv" link)
            (when (derived-mode-p 'elfeed-search-mode)
              (elfeed-search-untag-all-unread)))
        (message "No link found."))))

  (map! :map elfeed-search-mode-map
        :desc "Filter by feed"      "C-c f" #'my/elfeed-search-filter-by-feed
        :desc "Browse feed homepage" "B" #'my/elfeed-browse-homepage
        :desc "Toggle unread"       "R" #'my/elfeed-toggle-unread
        :desc "Play with mpv"       "v" #'my/elfeed-play-with-mpv)
  (map! :map elfeed-show-mode-map
        :desc "Browse feed homepage" "B" #'my/elfeed-browse-homepage
        :desc "Toggle unread"       "R" #'my/elfeed-toggle-unread
        :desc "Play with mpv"       "v" #'my/elfeed-play-with-mpv))

(use-package! elfeed-org
  :after elfeed
  :config
  (elfeed-org)
  ;; 2026-10-18 退役: notes.org 已并入 roam, elfeed 订阅独立成 roam 节点
  ;; (roam 节点 ID 20260101T000013, 84 条订阅, 逐条 diff 过与退役前一一对应 ——
  ;;  迁移时漏过一条 `***` 跳级的播客 feed, 已补回)。
  ;; elfeed-org 只认「标题以 URL 开头」的 heading; 全库 84 条全部在这一个文件里,
  ;; 所以直接指定它, 而不是递归 notes/ (media/read 里也有 URL, 扫进去会误收)。
  ;; 注意: 别在其他 roam 节点里写「标题以 http 开头」的普通笔记, 会被当成订阅源。
  ;;
  ;; ⚠ 2026-10-21 修复「M-x elfeed 打不开」:
  ;;   这里原本写死文件名 `20260101T000013--elfeed-subscriptions.org`, 但 10-19 那次
  ;;   全库 roam 重命名(去掉 `T` 分隔符、`--` 改 `_`、标题空格改下划线)之后, 实际文件
  ;;   已经是 `20260101000013-elfeed_subscriptions.org`。写死的路径随之失效, 而
  ;;   `rmh-elfeed-org-check-configuration-file` 对不存在的文件是直接 (error ...),
  ;;   它又被 `rmh-elfeed-org-process-advice` 挂在 `elfeed` 的 :before 上 ——
  ;;   结果就是**每次 M-x elfeed 都在启动前抛错, 界面根本出不来**。
  ;;   改成按文件名模式在 notes/ 下查, 不再依赖具体文件名, 下次重命名不会再炸。
  (defun my/elfeed-org-subscription-file ()
    "Locate the single org file that holds elfeed subscriptions.
Matched by filename pattern under `org-directory'/notes, so a notes file
rename (like the 2026-10-19 whole-library rename) cannot break elfeed again."
    (or (car (directory-files-recursively
              (expand-file-name "notes" org-directory)
              ;; 2026-10-24 改: 正则原来写作 `elfeed.*subscriptions?\.org\'',
              ;; 要求 `subscriptions' 后面**紧跟** .org。denote 迁移后文件名变成
              ;; `<ID>--elfeed_subscriptions__emacs.org', 中间插了个 __keywords 段,
              ;; 旧正则匹配不到 -> 退回兜底路径 -> 而兜底路径也随改名失效 ->
              ;; `rmh-elfeed-org-check-configuration-file' 直接 error, 挂在 elfeed
              ;; 的 :before advice 上 => **M-x elfeed 打不开** (复刻 10-21 事故)。
              ;; 放宽成 `subscription.*\.org\'', keywords 段插在中间也认。
              "elfeed.*subscription.*\\.org\\'"))
        ;; 2026-10-24 再改: denote 迁移把全库改名成 `<ID>--<title>__<kw>.org',
        ;; 兜底路径跟着换。它是**最后一道保险** —— 上面 glob 命中就用不到它。
        ;; 2026-10-25 改: linux/ emacs/ pkm/ software/ 四个域目录合并成 software/。
        ;; 上面那个 glob 递归扫整个 notes/, 合并前后都能命中 —— 这行兜底同步更新
        ;; 只是为了「万一哪天文件名也被改了, 兜底别失效」。
        (expand-file-name
         "notes/software/20260101T000013--elfeed_subscriptions__emacs.org"
         org-directory)))
  (setq rmh-elfeed-org-files (list (my/elfeed-org-subscription-file)))
  ;; 2026-10-25 修: elfeed-org 的 ignore tag 默认是 "ignore", 而订阅文件里一律用
  ;; `:@skip:' —— 两边从没对上过, 所有 @skip feed 一直在被抓取 (elfeed DB 里躺着
  ;; V2EX / reddit 等 @skip feed 即证据, 2026-10-25 实测 84 条全进 elfeed-feeds)。
  ;; 这一行让 `:@skip:' 真正生效: 失效 feed 打 `:@skip:' + 注释行标记后,
  ;; elfeed-org 不再把它加进 elfeed-feeds (README 的 ignore tag 是可配置的)。
  (setq rmh-elfeed-org-ignore-tag "@skip"))

;; ⚠ 2026-10-23 装 elfeed-goodies 时连修两处 (实测确认, 非推测):
;; 1) **必须显式调用 (elfeed-goodies/setup)**。函数名是 elfeed-goodies/setup
;;    (不是 elfeed-goodies —— 那样调用会 void-function); setup 带 ;;;###autoload,
;;    不调用则 elfeed-show-entry-switch / header 绘制 / n p 键位全部不生效,
;;    只 setq 变量 = 装了个空壳。
;; 2) **必须用 use-package! 而不是 after!**。after! 只是 with-eval-after-load
;;    包装, 不产生加载行为; 没有 use-package!/require 触发, 这个包永远不加载,
;;    整段配置静默失效。:after elfeed 保证 setup 跑在 elfeed-show-entry 之前
;;    (setup 设 entry-switch, RET 才用到), 同时保持懒加载不拖慢启动。
;; 布局叠加说明: setup 会把 entry-switch 换成 elfeed-goodies/switch-pane (走 popwin),
;; 上面 Elfeed 块的 set-popup-rule! 退为兜底 —— goodies 卸载时它重新接管。
;; 两者的方向一致 (right / 0.55), 实测左右分屏不变。
;; ⚠ **绝不要开 popwin-mode**: 它会重设 display-buffer-function 全局抢走所有
;; display-buffer 调用, Doom 的几十条 popup 规则 (*elfeed-entry*/*org-roam*/
;; capture 等) 全部失效。elfeed-goodies 的 split 路径不依赖 popwin-mode,
;; switch-pane 直接调 popwin:popup-buffer 建窗, 不开也能用 (2026-10-23 实测)。
(use-package! elfeed-goodies
  :after elfeed
  :config
  (setq elfeed-goodies/entry-pane-position 'right
        elfeed-goodies/entry-pane-size 0.55
        elfeed-goodies/switch-to-entry t
        elfeed-goodies/feed-source-column-width 28
        elfeed-goodies/tag-column-width 18)
  (elfeed-goodies/setup))

(after! elfeed-tube
  (setq elfeed-tube-auto-fetch-p t
        elfeed-tube-auto-save-p nil
        elfeed-tube-fields '(duration captions chapters description)
        elfeed-tube-backend 'yt-dlp))

(use-package! elfeed-tube-mpv
  :after elfeed-tube)
#+end_src
