** PowerShell
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package! powershell
  :mode ("\\.ps1\\'" . powershell-mode)
  :config
  (defun my/powershell-run-file ()
    "Run current .ps1 file."
    (interactive)
    (unless buffer-file-name (user-error "Buffer has no file"))
    (save-buffer)
    (compile (format "pwsh -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File \"%s\""
                     (expand-file-name buffer-file-name))))

  (defun my/powershell-run-region ()
    "Send region or current line to inferior PowerShell."
    (interactive)
    (let* ((beg (if (use-region-p) (region-beginning) (line-beginning-position)))
           (end (if (use-region-p) (region-end)       (line-end-position)))
           (code (buffer-substring-no-properties beg end)))
      (unless (get-buffer "*PowerShell*") (powershell))
      (comint-send-string (get-buffer-process "*PowerShell*") (concat code "\n"))
      (display-buffer "*PowerShell*")))

  (map! :map powershell-mode-map
        "C-c C-c" #'my/powershell-run-file
        "C-c C-r" #'my/powershell-run-region
        "C-c C-z" #'powershell))
#+end_src

* Session persistence (desktop)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; 会话持久化：记住上次的窗口布局 / 打开的文件 / 光标位置，下次启动自动恢复。
;; 配合 Windows 登录自启（HKCU Run → runemacs.exe）使用。
;; 想临时不恢复：runemacs.exe --no-desktop
(let ((dir (expand-file-name "desktop/" doom-cache-dir)))
  (unless (file-directory-p dir)
    (make-directory dir t))
  (setq desktop-dirname        dir
        desktop-path           (list dir)
        desktop-base-file-name ".emacs.desktop"
        desktop-base-lock-name ".emacs.desktop.lock"))

(setq desktop-save                       t   ; 退出时无条件保存，不弹问询
      desktop-load-locked-desktop        t   ; 上次异常退出留下的 lock 也直接加载
      desktop-auto-save-timeout          300 ; 空闲 5 分钟自动存一次（防强杀 / 断电）
      desktop-restore-frames             t   ; 恢复 frame 位置、大小、分屏
      desktop-restore-reuses-frames      t
      desktop-restore-forces-onscreen    t
      desktop-restore-in-current-display nil
      desktop-lazy-idle-delay            3   ; 后台分批把剩余 buffer 加载完
      desktop-lazy-verbose               nil)

;; 内部 / 临时 buffer 不写进 desktop（保留默认值，再追加）
(setq desktop-buffers-not-to-save
      (concat "\\` "
              "\\|\\*\\(straight-process\\|straight-byte-compile\\|doom\\|doom:\\|"
              "Messages\\|Async.*\\|ielm\\|e?shell.*\\|vterm.*\\|PowerShell\\|"
              "compilation\\|Org .*\\)\\*"
              "\\|\\.log\\'"))

;; 2026-10-22 停用：会话恢复交给 easysession（见下一节）。
;; 两者功能重叠且会各自按自己的顺序抢着摆窗口布局，不能同时开。
;; 上面这段配置完整保留，方便回滚 —— 想退回 desktop 只要改回 `(desktop-save-mode 1)'
;; 并把下一节的 easysession 块注释掉。旧的 ~/.emacs.d/.local/cache/desktop/
;; .emacs.desktop 也留着不动（desktop 停用后不再更新，留作回滚时的备份）。
(desktop-save-mode -1)
#+end_src

* Session & buffers (2026-10-22)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;;; Session & buffers
;; 来源：jamescherti《Essential Emacs Packages》清单评审。整套 90 多项，
;; 扣掉写代码向的（LSP / 格式化 / 折叠 / 各语言 mode）和 Doom 已自带的，
;; 剩下就是这三个。

;; persist-text-scale: 文字缩放跨会话保持。
;; Doom 的 text-zoom（SPC z x / SPC z z）是纯 buffer-local，重开 Emacs 就回到
;; 默认值；这个 minor mode 把每个文件的 text-scale-mode-amount 落盘持久化。
(use-package! persist-text-scale
  :config
  (persist-text-scale-mode 1))

;; buffer-terminator: 自动回收长时间闲置的 buffer。
;; 动机：roam 361 个 + daily 236 个文件，会话挂久了 SPC b b 会被几百个 buffer
;; 淹没；而且每个 buffer 都拖着自己的 buffer-local mode 和 timer，
;; Emacs 单线程，timer 数量上去了直接反映成卡顿。
;; 保守取值：45 分钟无交互才回收（默认 30），每 15 分钟扫一次（默认 10）。
;; kill 前会检查未保存修改、进程 buffer 等，默认规则足够安全；
;; 真要更细的保留/回收策略，改 buffer-terminator-rules-alist。
(use-package! buffer-terminator
  :config
  (setq buffer-terminator-verbose            nil
        buffer-terminator-inactivity-timeout (* 45 60)
        buffer-terminator-interval           (* 15 60))
  (buffer-terminator-mode 1))

(use-package! easysession
  :demand t
  :config
  ;; 会话文件放 ~/.emacs.d/.local/cache/easysession/，与 desktop/ 同级且同样被 gitignore
  (setq easysession-directory (expand-file-name "easysession/" doom-cache-dir)

        ;; ⚠ 默认是 nil —— 不设的话只在「退出 Emacs」时保存一次，
        ;;   崩溃 / 强杀 / systemctl kill 就全丢。这里对齐 desktop 的
        ;;   desktop-auto-save-timeout 300，改成 10 分钟一次。
        easysession-save-interval (* 10 60)

        ;; 启动时自动加载上次会话（等价于 desktop-read）
        easysession-setup-load-session t

        ;; 切换会话前先存当前会话
        easysession-switch-to-save-session t
        easysession-switch-to-exclude-current nil

        ;; 记录所有打开的 buffer（= desktop 的行为，也是包的默认值）。
        ;; 2026-10-23 更正：前一天我设成了 #'easysession-visible-buffer-list
        ;; （只记看得见的），理由是让它跟 buffer-terminator 同向 —— 结果是重启后
        ;; 140 个文件一个都没回来，只能靠 M-x desktop-read 手工救。
        ;; 实测你要的就是「全回来」，别再为了跟别的包讲和而降级这里。
        easysession-buffer-list-function #'buffer-list)

  ;; 入口是 setup，不是 save-mode：它负责挂 emacs-startup-hook（daemon 下是
  ;; server-after-make-frame-hook）做启动自动加载 + 定期保存 + 退出时保存。
  (easysession-setup))

;; 常用命令（暂时不加 leader 键位，先看一段时间哪些真的用得上）：
;;   M-x easysession-switch-to                       切换会话
;;   M-x easysession-switch-to-and-restore-geometry  切换并恢复窗口几何
;;   M-x easysession-save / -rename / -delete / -unload
;;   M-x easysession-reset                           清空 buffer / frame / window
#+end_src

* Email (mu4e + mbsync)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;;; Email (mu4e + mbsync)
;;
;; 外部依赖（系统级，必须用 root 装，Doom 装不了）：
;;   Arch/Omarchy:  sudo pacman -S isync  &&  yay -S mu
;;                  （mu 不在官方仓库，只有 AUR；isync 提供 mbsync）
;;   Debian/Ubuntu: sudo apt install isync maildir-utils
;;     isync          → mbsync，负责 IMAP 双向同步
;;     mu / maildir-utils → mu（索引/查询）；mu4e（Emacs 界面 elisp）由 Doom 装
;;
;; 数据流：  邮件服务器 --mbsync--> ~/.mail/{gmail,outlook} (Maildir)
;;                                  --mu index--> Xapian 索引 --mu4e--> Emacs
;;
;; 密码放在 ~/.authinfo.gpg（GPG 加密，直接用 emacs 打开编辑，保存自动重加密）。
;; mbsync 通过 ~/.local/bin/authinfo-get 解密取密码 —— 配置文件里没有明文。
;; 配置文件：
;;   ~/.mbsyncrc              IMAP 同步规则（Gmail + Outlook 两个 Channel）
;;   ~/.authinfo.gpg          凭据
;;   ~/.local/bin/authinfo-get 解密取密码的小脚本
;;
;; 首次使用流程（按顺序跑一次）：
;;   1. emacs ~/.authinfo.gpg   把 CHANGE_ME_GMAIL / CHANGE_ME_OUTLOOK 换成应用专用密码
;;   2. mkdir -p ~/.mail/gmail ~/.mail/outlook
;;   3. mbsync --all            首次全量同步，几万封邮件可能要几十分钟
;;   4. mu init --maildir ~/.mail \
;;          --my-address yuanxiang424@gmail.com \
;;          --my-address yuan_xiang@outlook.com
;;      mu index
;;   5. SPC o m 打开 mu4e（SPC o M 写新邮件）
;;
;; 日常：mu4e 每 5 分钟自动跑一次 mbsync --all + mu index。

;; Ubuntu 把 mu4e 的 elisp 装到 /usr/share/emacs/site-lisp/mu4e，
;; 而自编的 Emacs 31（prefix=/usr/local）不会自动把 /usr/share/... 放进 load-path，
;; 不补这一行 (require 'mu4e) 直接失败、整个模块静默哑火。
(dolist (dir (append (file-expand-wildcards "/usr/share/emacs/site-lisp/*mu4e*")
                     (file-expand-wildcards "/usr/local/share/emacs/site-lisp/*mu4e*")))
  (when (file-directory-p dir)
    (add-to-list 'load-path dir)))

(after! mu4e
  (setq mu4e-root-maildir "~/.mail")
  (when (boundp 'mu4e-maildir)
    (setq mu4e-maildir "~/.mail"))

  (setq
   ;; mbsync 移动邮件会改文件名，mu4e 必须跟着改，否则索引与文件对不上
   mu4e-change-filenames-when-moving t
   ;; 每 5 分钟自动 mbsync + reindex；嫌吵改 nil，然后手动 U 更新
   mu4e-update-interval 300
   mu4e-get-mail-command "mbsync --all"
   ;; Gmail 用标签而不是文件夹，邮件不会真的"移动"，可以做懒检查
   mu4e-index-cleanup nil
   mu4e-index-lazy-check t
   ;; 收件人显示为名字而不是一长串地址
   mu4e-view-show-addresses t
   mu4e-compose-dont-reply-to-self t
   mu4e-user-mail-address-list '("yuanxiang424@gmail.com"
                                 "yuan_xiang@outlook.com"))

  ;; +gmail flag 靠 maildir 根名含 "gmail" 自动识别；这里显式登记一遍更保险
  (setq +mu4e-gmail-accounts '(("yuanxiang424@gmail.com" . "/gmail")))

  (setq mu4e-contexts
        (list
         ;; ---------------- Gmail ----------------
         (make-mu4e-context
          :name "Gmail"
          :match-func (lambda (msg)
                        (when msg
                          (string-prefix-p "/gmail"
                                           (mu4e-message-field msg :maildir))))
          :vars '((user-mail-address     . "yuanxiang424@gmail.com")
                  (user-full-name        . "Sean Yuan")
                  (mu4e-sent-folder      . "/gmail/[Gmail]/Sent Mail")
                  (mu4e-drafts-folder    . "/gmail/[Gmail]/Drafts")
                  (mu4e-trash-folder     . "/gmail/[Gmail]/Trash")
                  ;; Gmail 没有真正的"归档"目录，/gmail/Archive 是一个自建标签。
                  ;; mbsync 把它 CREATE 到服务器上（Gmail 会变成一个 label），
                  ;; 移进去就等于去掉 INBOX 标签 = 归档。若 Gmail 拒绝建目录，
                  ;; 改成 "/gmail/[Gmail]/All Mail"（代价是索引里出现重复邮件）。
                  (mu4e-refile-folder    . "/gmail/Archive")
                  (smtpmail-smtp-server  . "smtp.gmail.com")
                  (smtpmail-smtp-service . 587)
                  (smtpmail-stream-type  . starttls)
                  (smtpmail-smtp-user    . "yuanxiang424@gmail.com")))
         ;; --------------- Outlook ---------------
         (make-mu4e-context
          :name "Outlook"
          :match-func (lambda (msg)
                        (when msg
                          (string-prefix-p "/outlook"
                                           (mu4e-message-field msg :maildir))))
          :vars '((user-mail-address     . "yuan_xiang@outlook.com")
                  (user-full-name        . "Sean Yuan")
                  ;; Outlook 个人账号的目录名取决于网页界面语言：
                  ;; 英文是 Sent/Drafts/Deleted，中文界面可能是「已发送邮件」等。
                  ;; 跑完 mbsync --all 后 ls ~/.mail/outlook 看实际名字再改。
                  (mu4e-sent-folder      . "/outlook/Sent")
                  (mu4e-drafts-folder    . "/outlook/Drafts")
                  (mu4e-trash-folder     . "/outlook/Deleted")
                  (mu4e-refile-folder    . "/outlook/Archive")
                  (smtpmail-smtp-server  . "smtp-mail.outlook.com")
                  (smtpmail-smtp-service . 587)
                  (smtpmail-stream-type  . starttls)
                  (smtpmail-smtp-user    . "yuan_xiang@outlook.com")))))

  (setq mu4e-bookmarks
        '((:name "未读"            :query "flag:unread AND NOT flag:trashed" :key ?u)
          (:name "今天"            :query "date:today..now"                  :key ?t)
          (:name "最近 7 天"        :query "date:7d..now"                     :key ?w)
          (:name "Gmail 收件箱"     :query "maildir:/gmail/INBOX"             :key ?g)
          (:name "Outlook 收件箱"   :query "maildir:/outlook/INBOX"           :key ?o)
          (:name "带附件"          :query "mime:application/* OR mime:image/*" :key ?a)
          (:name "Gmail 已发送"     :query "maildir:\"/gmail/[Gmail]/Sent Mail\"" :key ?G)
          (:name "Outlook 已发送"   :query "maildir:/outlook/Sent"            :key ?O))))

;; 有些邮件服务商（含 Outlook）发的 HTML 邮件肉眼没法看，优先看 text/plain
(with-eval-after-load 'mm-decode
  (setq mm-discouraged-alternatives '("text/html" "text/richtext")
        mm-text-html-renderer 'shr))
#+end_src

* Keybindings
** Leader
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(map! :leader
      ;; Gcal
      (:prefix ("G" . "gcal")
       :desc "Sync"   "s" #'org-gcal-sync
       :desc "Fetch"  "f" #'org-gcal-fetch
       :desc "Delete" "d" #'org-gcal-delete-at-point
       :desc "Push"   "p" #'my/org-gcal-auto-post)

      ;; Open
      (:prefix ("o" . "open")
       :desc "Elfeed"           "e" #'elfeed
       :desc "Eshell here"      "E" #'my/eshell-here
       :desc "Daily agenda"     "d" (cmd! (org-agenda nil "d"))
       :desc "Weekly agenda"    "w" (cmd! (org-agenda nil "w"))
       :desc "GTD review"       "g" (cmd! (org-agenda nil "g"))
       :desc "Beancount ledger" "$" #'my/open-beancount-main
       :desc "EPUB reader"      "R" #'my/epub-reader-open-dwim
       :desc "Mail (mu4e)"      "m" #'mu4e
       :desc "Compose mail"     "M" #'mu4e-compose-new)

      ;; Notes（denote 前缀 2026-10-17 删除，journal 两个键收进这里）
      (:prefix ("n" . "notes")
       :desc "Consult notes (pick)"   "s" #'consult-notes
       ;; 2026-10-22 换自家版本: 上游的会把 org-roam-directory 整个目录塞进 rg,
       ;; 236 个 daily 密文刷屏。自家版用文件列表, 覆盖一致但排除 daily。
       ;; (2026-10-24 晚: 日记已取消加密, 但仍排除 —— 用 my/journal-search
       ;;  专门搜日记更快, 见下面 J。)
       :desc "Consult notes (search)" "S" #'my/consult-notes-search-in-all-notes
       ;; 2026-10-22 换自家版本: 上游 `org-ql-find-in-org-directory' 不递归,
       ;; 实测只搜到 ~/org/inbox.org 一个文件。自家版走 `my/org-ql-files'。
       :desc "Org-QL find · 全库(排日记)" "q" #'my/org-ql-find-notes
       :desc "Today's journal"         "j" #'my/journal-today
       ;; 2026-10-22 加: Doom 的 dailies 键 `SPC m m d' 只在 org buffer 里能用,
       ;; 这里补一个全局入口 -> `my/journal-goto-date' (不存在提示, 不新建)。
       ;; 2026-10-24 改: 键位 d -> **g**。d 让给 denote 前缀 (`SPC n d',
       ;; d = denote), 这里改用 g = goto, 与描述里的「跳到」也对得上。
       :desc "Goto journal date · 跳到某天" "g" #'my/journal-goto-date
       :desc "Insert weather · 补天气" "W" #'my/journal-insert-weather
       ;; C-u 前缀 = 只搜最近 90 天
       ;; (2026-10-24 晚: 原注释说「全量要解密 236 个文件, 实测 ~40s」——
       ;;  日记取消加密后是纯文本 I/O, 毫秒级, 全量搜也不卡了)
       :desc "Search journal · 搜日记"  "J" #'my/journal-search
       ;; 2026-10-24 晚: 接上 denote-journal 的原生命令。
       ;; N = new (新建今天的日记, 走 denote-journal 规范命名)
       ;; E = new-or-existing (当天已有就打开, 没有就建) —— 日常最常用这个
       ;; L = link-or-create (从当前文件链到今天的日记, 没有就后台建一篇)
       :desc "Journal new · 新建日记"     "N" #'denote-journal-new-entry
       :desc "Journal today · 今天的"     "E" #'denote-journal-new-or-existing-entry
       :desc "Journal link · 链到日记"     "L" #'denote-journal-link-or-create-entry
       (:prefix ("i" . "investment · 投资")
        :desc "Beancount buy · 买入"                  "b" #'my/beancount-record-buy
        :desc "Beancount sell · 卖出"                 "s" #'my/beancount-record-sell
        :desc "Beancount prices · 批量记录价格"        "p" #'my/beancount-record-price-batch
        :desc "Beancount price · 单个记录价格"         "P" #'my/beancount-record-price
        :desc "Beancount balance · 持仓校验"          "B" #'my/beancount-record-balance
        :desc "Beancount balances · 批量持仓校验"     "V" #'my/beancount-record-balance-batch
        :desc "Update prices latest · 更新近期价格"   "u" #'my/beancount-update-prices-latest
        :desc "Rebuild price history · 重建历史价格"  "H" #'my/beancount-rebuild-prices-history
        :desc "Open ledger · 打开账本"                "o" #'my/open-beancount-main
        :desc "Check ledger · 检查账本"               "c" #'my/beancount-check-main
        :desc "Fava · 打开报表"                       "f" #'my/beancount-fava-main
        :desc "Bank wealth redeem · 银行理财赎回"     "r" #'my/beancount-record-bank-redeem
        :desc "Pension fund buy · 养老金基金买入"     "y" #'my/beancount-record-pension-buy
        :desc "CMB cashflow · 招行转入转出"          "C" #'my/beancount-record-cmb-cashflow
        :desc "Bank interest · 银行结息收入"         "i" #'my/beancount-record-bank-interest
        :desc "Collateral transfer · 担保品划转"      "t" #'my/beancount-record-collateral-transfer
        :desc "Margin buy · 融资买入"                "m" #'my/beancount-record-margin-buy
        :desc "Margin sell · 融资卖出"               "M" #'my/beancount-record-margin-sell)
       (:prefix ("n" . "capture · 速记")
        :desc "Task · 任务"        "t" (cmd! (org-capture nil "t"))
        :desc "Inbox · 收件箱"     "i" (cmd! (org-capture nil "i"))
        :desc "Journal · 日志"     "j" (cmd! (org-capture nil "j"))
        :desc "Append · 追加"      "a" (cmd! (org-capture nil "a"))
        (:prefix ("m" . "media · 影音")
         :desc "电影·想看"  "m" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mm"))
         :desc "电影·看完"  "M" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mM"))
         :desc "剧·想看"    "t" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mt"))
         :desc "剧·看完"    "T" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mT"))
         :desc "动漫·想看"  "a" (cmd! (org-capture nil "ma"))
         :desc "动漫·看完"  "A" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mA")))
        (:prefix ("b" . "books · 书")
         :desc "书·待读"    "b" (cmd! (org-capture nil "bb"))
         :desc "书·阅读中"  "B" (cmd! (org-capture nil "bB"))))
       ;; 2026-10-18: Doom 官方把 roam 键位绑在 org-mode-map 的 localleader（`SPC m m`），
       ;; 只在 org 文件里有效；在 dashboard / scratch / 任何非 org buffer 里会是
       ;; "SPC m m is undefined"。这里补一组**全局** leader 键位，任何地方都能唤起。
       ;;
       ;; ⚠ 2026-10-22 修正: 这段原先缩进在 `(:prefix ("n" . "capture · 速记"))` **内部**,
       ;;   实际路径是 `SPC n n r' 而不是设想的 `SPC n r' (实测后者 UNBOUND)。
       ;;   （2026-10-24 注: 这里的 r 是**当时的**键位，今天这个前缀是 `SPC n d'。
       ;;     历史记录不改，免得和当年的实测对不上。）
       ;;   `map!' 的嵌套层级由括号结构决定 —— 带描述的 :prefix 只影响同级键的保留与否,
       ;;   不会改变层级; 错一级就整体下沉一层, 且不报任何错。改完必须实测 key-binding,
       ;;   不能只看 tangle 通过。
       ;; 2026-10-24 二改: **前缀键 r -> d**。`r' 是 org-roam 时代的前缀
       ;; （Doom 的 `+roam' 就是这么绑的），org-roam 已经退役，前缀名与键位都
       ;; 该换掉。d = denote —— 与 `SPC n d f/c/i/b' 这组主操作对得上。
       ;; 代价: journal 的「跳到某天」原先占着 d，已挪到 `SPC n g'（g = goto）。
       ;;
       ;; ⚠ 描述文字同样在去 org-roam 化: denote 的世界里叫 **note** 不叫 node,
       ;; 也没有 "capture" 这个概念（那是 org-roam 的话）。所以
       ;;   "Find node · 找节点"     -> "Find note · 找笔记"
       ;;   "Capture · 新建节点"     -> "New note · 新建笔记"
       ;;   "Extract · heading 成节点" -> "Extract · heading 成笔记"
       ;;
       ;; 旧 org-roam 命令 → 现在的实现（历史对照，留着好回溯）:
       ;;   f org-roam-node-find      → denote-open-or-create（找不到就新建，同手感）
       ;;   i org-roam-node-insert    → denote-org-link-to-heading（可带 heading）
       ;;   b org-roam-buffer-toggle  → denote-org-backlinks-for-heading
       ;;   c org-roam-capture        → denote（按 denote-prompts 走 title/kw/子目录）
       ;;   g org-roam-graph          → **删除**（denote 无图谱，且本库不靠图导航）
       ;;   s org-roam-db-sync        → org-id-update-id-locations（[[id:]] 的真索引）
       ;;   t my/org-roam-tag-add-known → org-set-tags-command（候选=org-tag-alist
       ;;                                 =my/known-keywords 白名单，原生就够）
       ;;   T org-roam-tag-remove     → org-set-tags-command（清空即可删）
       ;;   x my/org-roam-extract-to-domain → denote-org-extract-org-subtree
       ;;                                 （领域目录由 denote-prompts 的
       ;;                                  subdirectory 提示接管，不用再写补丁）
       (:prefix ("d" . "denote · 笔记")
        :desc "Find note · 找笔记"   "f" #'denote-open-or-create
        ;; 2026-10-25: i / I 拆分。denote-org-link-to-heading 永远产 heading 级链接
        ;; ([[denote:ID::#custom-id]])；整篇笔记级链接走 denote-link ([[denote:ID]])。
        ;; ⚠ 绑 denote-link 不绑 denote-insert-link：后者只是 defalias，无 autoload
        ;; cookie，绑它 byte-compile 会多条 "not known to be defined" 警告。
        :desc "Insert link · 插链接"    "i" #'denote-link
        :desc "Link heading · 链到标题" "I" #'denote-org-link-to-heading
        :desc "Backlinks · 反链"     "b" #'denote-org-backlinks-for-heading
        :desc "New note · 新建笔记"  "c" #'denote
        :desc "Rebuild id index · 重建索引" "s" #'org-id-update-id-locations
        ;; 2026-10-22 加 / 2026-10-24 改: tag 入口。候选来自 `org-tag-alist'，
        ;; 而后者在 Refile 块里被设成 `my/known-keywords'（27 个）—— 白名单天然生效，
        ;; 不再需要 my/org-roam-tag-add-known 那层包装。remove = 清空重设。
        :desc "Add tag · 加标签"    "t" #'org-set-tags-command
        :desc "Remove tag · 删标签" "T" #'org-set-tags-command
        ;; 2026-10-21 加 / 2026-10-24 改: 抽取 subtree 成独立笔记。
        ;; denote-prompts 含 subdirectory，抽取时会自己问领域目录。
        :desc "Extract · heading 成笔记" "x" #'denote-org-extract-org-subtree
        ;; 2026-10-23 加: 网页存档。URL 从剪贴板取 (elfeed/eww 里直接按 L)。
        :desc "Literature · 网页存档"   "w" #'my/literature-save
        ;; 2026-10-23: 外部浏览器里看到想存的两个入口。W = 剪贴板富文本 (不联网,
        ;; 反爬站点可用), S = Ctrl+S 另存的 HTML (最保真, 连图都在)。
        :desc "Literature · 剪贴板网页" "W" #'my/literature-save-clipboard
        :desc "Literature · 本地 HTML"  "S" #'my/literature-save-file))

      ;; Toggle / utilities
      (:prefix ("t" . "toggle")
       ;; :desc "Focus writing"         "F" #'my/focus-writing-toggle
       )

      :desc "Visual undo tree" "u" #'vundo
      :desc "org-noter"        "N" #'org-noter)
#+end_src

** Org localleader
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(map! :after org
      :map org-mode-map
      :localleader
      (:prefix ("i" . "image")
       :desc "Preview near point" "t" #'my/org-preview-images-near-point
       :desc "Attach menu"    "a" #'my/org-attach-menu
       ;; p / s 走 org-download, 但 org-download-method = 'attach, 所以
       ;; 一样落到 org-attach 目录 + [[attachment:]] 链接
       :desc "Paste clipboard" "p" #'org-download-clipboard
       :desc "Screenshot"     "s" #'org-download-screenshot
       :desc "Yank"           "y" #'org-download-yank
       :desc "Delete file"   "d" #'my/org-delete-file-link-at-point
       :desc "Rotate file"  "r" #'my/org-rotate-image-file
       :desc "Open external" "o" #'my/org-image-open-external
       :desc "Zoom in"       "+" #'my/org-image-resize-up
       :desc "Zoom out"      "-" #'my/org-image-resize-down
       :desc "Caption"       "c" #'my/org-image-caption
       :desc "Preview links" "l" #'org-link-preview
       :desc "Compress→webp" "O" #'my/org-image-optimize
       :desc "Grab http img" "D" #'my/org-image-download-link-at-point
       :desc "Insert figure" "f" #'my/org-insert-image-figure
       :desc "Dedupe imgs"   "x" #'my/org-dedupe-inline-images
       :desc "Number captions" "n" #'my/org-caption-number-mode)
      ;; NOTE: Doom 自带 (:prefix ("d" . "date/deadline"))，带描述的 :prefix 会
      ;; 清空该前缀原有键，所以 date 那几个键必须在这里显式补回来。
      ;; 2026-10-17: denote 那 6 个键 + dblock 子组随包一起删除, 前缀改名 date/id。
      ;; `I' 是大文件体系下加 heading CUSTOM_ID 的命令, 跟 denote 无关, 保留。
      (:prefix ("d" . "date/id")
       ;; org date/deadline（原 Doom 默认，勿删）
       :desc "Schedule"               "s" #'org-schedule
       :desc "Deadline"               "d" #'org-deadline
       :desc "Timestamp"              "t" #'org-time-stamp
       :desc "Inactive timestamp"     "T" #'org-time-stamp-inactive
       ;; 大文件体系下的 heading 级 ID (C-u 前缀 = 同时加进顶部导航)
       :desc "Set heading CUSTOM_ID"  "I" #'my/org-heading-id)
      ;; org-transclusion
      (:prefix ("T" . "transclusion")
       :desc "Add at point"    "a" #'org-transclusion-add
       :desc "Add all"         "A" #'org-transclusion-add-all
       :desc "Remove at point" "r" #'org-transclusion-remove
       :desc "Toggle mode"     "t" #'org-transclusion-mode)
      :desc "TODO state"         "t" #'org-todo
      :desc "Paste rich text"    "V" #'my/org-paste-rich
      :desc "Focus writing"      "F" #'my/focus-writing-toggle
      :desc "Rebucket entry"     "R" #'my/org-rebucket-current-entry
      :desc "Archive done tasks" "A" #'my/org-archive-done-tasks)
#+end_src

* Late fixes
** C-c C-c in =*Org Note*= popup
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defadvice! my/org-ctrl-c-ctrl-c-note-fix (fn &rest args)
  :around #'org-ctrl-c-ctrl-c
  (if (string= (buffer-name) "*Org Note*")
      (org-store-log-note)
    (apply fn args)))
#+end_src

** Smart Input Source (sis) + Rime — Windows
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(when (featurep :system 'windows)
  (use-package! sis
    :demand t
    :config
    ;; Skip sis's auto-detect; wire Windows IME API directly.
    (setq sis-english-source nil
          sis-other-source   t
          sis-do-get         #'w32-get-ime-open-status
          sis-do-set         #'w32-set-ime-open-status)

    ;; Disable 0.2s idle poll (races imm32.dll on buffer creation).
    (setq sis-auto-refresh-seconds nil)

    (defun sis-switch ()
      "Switch between English and other, driven by the Windows IME API."
      (interactive)
      (setq sis--for-buffer-locked nil)
      (if (w32-get-ime-open-status)
          (sis--set-english)
        (sis--set-other)))

    (sis-global-cursor-color-mode t)
    (when (fboundp 'modus-themes-get-color-value)
      (setq sis-other-cursor-color   (modus-themes-get-color-value 'fg-alt)
            sis-default-cursor-color (modus-themes-get-color-value 'border)))

    (sis-global-respect-mode t)
    (sis-global-context-mode t)
    (sis-global-inline-mode  t)

    (setq sis-respect-go-english-triggers (list #'org-agenda))

    ;; Resync after frame regains focus
    (add-function :after after-focus-change-function
                  (lambda () (sis--get)))

    :hook
    (org-capture-mode . sis-set-other))

  ;; Bind the C-backslash key everywhere evil might grab keys first.
  ;; (注释里**不能**写裸反斜杠: Emacs reader 会把反斜杠+分号当转义序列,
  ;;  于是这个注释不结束, 后面的代码全被吞进注释, 最后报 "end-of-file"。
  ;;  org-babel tangle 不改转义层数, 但这个坑在 .el 里一样致命。)
  (define-key global-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
  (with-eval-after-load 'evil
    (define-key evil-normal-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-insert-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-motion-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-visual-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-emacs-state-map  (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)))
#+end_src

** Smart Input Source (sis) + Rime — Linux (fcitx5)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(when (and (featurep :system 'linux)
           (executable-find "fcitx5-remote"))
  (use-package! sis
    :demand t
    :config
    ;; fcitx5-remote prints "1" (inactive → keyboard-us) or "2" (active → rime).
    ;; `sis-ism-lazyman-config' points sis--ism at the fcitx5-remote binary and
    ;; wires do-set to -c / -o; do-get is derived from the same binary.
    (sis-ism-lazyman-config "1" "2" 'fcitx5)

    ;; 不轮询。sis 的 refresh 读的是 fcitx5 的**全局**状态 —— fcitx5-remote
    ;; 无参数时查的是当前焦点 IC, 而 Emacs 失焦时那是**别的窗口**的状态。
    ;; sis 拿到这个状态会按它 set 回去, 于是"不打字也会自己切英文":
    ;; 每秒一次读 + 写, 外部窗口的英文状态被反复灌进 fcitx5。
    ;; (实测: 30 秒里 21 次 State + 4 次 Deactivate, 全部来自这个 idle timer。)
    ;; 与 Windows 分支保持一致 (那里早就设 nil, 见上方)。
    ;; 代价: Emacs 不再自动感知外部切换 (如 Super+Space), 需手动切一次。
    (setq sis-auto-refresh-seconds nil)

    (sis-global-cursor-color-mode t)
    (when (fboundp 'modus-themes-get-color-value)
      (setq sis-other-cursor-color   (modus-themes-get-color-value 'fg-alt)
            sis-default-cursor-color (modus-themes-get-color-value 'border)))

    (sis-global-respect-mode t)
    (sis-global-context-mode t)
    (sis-global-inline-mode  t)

    (setq sis-respect-go-english-triggers (list #'org-agenda))

    ;; Resync after frame regains focus
    (add-function :after after-focus-change-function
                  (lambda () (sis--get)))

    :hook
    (org-capture-mode . sis-set-other))

  ;; NOTE: deliberately duplicating the Windows bindings above rather than
  ;; hoisting them into a shared helper — keeps either platform's block from
  ;; breaking the other. Fold them together once both sides are settled.
  (define-key global-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
  (with-eval-after-load 'evil
    (define-key evil-normal-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-insert-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-motion-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-visual-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-emacs-state-map  (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)))
#+end_src
